# Project recovery

Handoff for the Kutadgu Bilig bookstore repository. Read this before substantial work, then compare it with `origin/main`. Incident rollback is `STAGE11_RECOVERY.md`. The one-page emergency list is `docs/EMERGENCY.md`.

The runtime baseline below is the last verified application commit. It is not automatically the current GitHub `main` tip. Documentation-only commits, including this handoff, do not move it.

The runtime baseline is `b11e205d89a26d8f392f9793e6af3e5f29d0c7aa`, the PR #337 merge. This documentation commit does not move that baseline. Stage 137 SQL stays applied. Production Worker version `7a471d8e-436b-4f4a-ac1f-fa18cedbec10`, deployment `480e4417-8f96-4f76-9384-9b7101fab45b`, at 100%, created 2026-10-26T23:32:57.071972Z, serves that merge. The rollback target recorded immediately before that deploy was version `8c96abf0-2189-44f1-84dd-402a44cca86b`, deployment `0a25ed53-a9fd-4d9d-8387-9b09b8977db4`, created 2026-10-26T22:57:03.897984Z. No SQL was applied for this release.

## Ordinary book description limit — draft

The ordinary «كىتاب ھەققىدە» field was capped at 1,200 characters only by `maxlength="1200"` on Admin `#bookDescription`. That attribute counts JavaScript string length, UTF-16 code units, including spaces and line breaks. The same field is used for create, edit, and pending staff-submission edit. Staff submission used `#staffDescription` with `maxlength="20000"`. `submit_book_for_approval` still stores `left(..., 20000)`. Pending-edit SQL stores the trimmed description with no length check. `books.description` is `text`. Series and volume-group descriptions stay at 10,000 characters. No database change is required to store 2,000 characters, and none was applied. A table check was not added, because it could reject a later update of an existing longer staff description.

Admin and staff now use that same raw string length, show a `n / 2,000` counter, and reject 2,001 characters with «كىتاب ھەققىدە 2,000 ھەرپتىن ئېشىپ كەتمىسۇن.» without clearing the field. Saved text is still trimmed. Branch `cursor/book-description-limit-3fc0`. The limit change is `865d6e7f8289d43e0b4b99948c323c16f565fb02`. Pins are `admin.js?v=125` and `book-staff.js?v=13`. `node scripts/book-description-limit-tests.js` passed the static checks and the Chromium checks: a shorter Uyghur description with a paragraph break saves, 2,000 characters save and reopen unchanged, and 2,001 characters are rejected with the field left intact. The staff payload case in `scripts/book-staff-portal-tests.js` passed. `node scripts/ci-asset-pin-preflight.js` passed. This draft does not move the runtime baseline. No SQL execution, production write, or deployment.

## Admin SDK global — released

The vendored Supabase UMD assigns `createClient` to `window.supabase` only when neither `module` nor `exports` is an object. If both exist, it writes the client to `module.exports` and leaves the window global empty. Later deferred admin scripts then replace `module.exports`. `admin.js` finds no `createClient` and shows the setup panel with «Supabase JavaScript كۈتۈپخانىسى يۈكلەنمىدى», while both script URLs still return 200 and the browser reports no script error. Enforced CSP remains `frame-ancestors 'none'`; the report-only script policy does not block the same-origin library. The PR #335 diff did not move that script or the startup guard. `admin-supabase-global.js?v=1` is deferred immediately after `/vendor/supabase-js-2.45.4.umd.js` and copies a `createClient` export onto `window.supabase` before later scripts replace it. Authentication, AAL2, configuration, and the book editor are unchanged. PR #336 merged as `45ee71dacafd9241500e46190c4c556854f8e0f5`. Its parents are `becabdb3fb0fe05730509a1fdd17e457ccca95dd` and `4efc7802474352b298b4a19ebbfe313c5b813755`. Its tree is `a5ed3f54c1513634fc09f5ed441d8807585f1a6d`, the same tree as the reviewed head. Stage 10 run [40414202317](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40414202317) succeeded on that head and was not restarted. `wrangler deploy --env production --keep-vars` used Wrangler 4.145.0 from a clean checkout of the merge. The upload was `admin.html` and `admin-supabase-global.js`. Bindings stayed `ASSETS` and `COVERS` (`kutadgu-covers-preview`). `KUTADGU_HOST_MODE` stayed `production` and `MULTI_VOLUME_BOOKS_ENABLED` stayed `true`. Existing production variables stayed. No secret value was printed or changed. Both custom domains remained attached. Production `admin.html` returns 200 with `Cache-Control: no-store` and requests the bridge immediately after the UMD. Both files match the merge byte for byte. Installed Chrome, signed out, with no auth bypass: without CommonJS globals and with `module`/`exports` defined, `#loginPanel` is visible, `#setupPanel` stays hidden, `createClient` is a function, and there are no startup page errors. No Admin sign-in was performed. No SQL.

## Saved member book editing — released

A saved series member had a public «كىتابنى ئېچىش» link and no way into the existing book editor, so a missing `books.pages` value could not be added without deleting the book. Each saved member now has «كىتاب ئۇچۇرىنى تەھرىرلەش». It calls the existing `openEdit(id)` UPDATE path. It does not insert a book, detach a member, or rebuild the series form. `admin_get_book_volume_group(bigint)` members omit `pages` and ISBN. After the group is drawn, the editor reads `id,pages` from `books` for the open group and paints only that page count. A read is ignored when the editor generation or the open group has changed, and a newer book save keeps its own page count. The read does not rewrite group fields, labels, order, files, membership, price, stock, or publication state. A successful update still refreshes that member's title, author, price, stock, ISBN, cover, active flag, and page count on the open series only. Pins are `admin.css?v=73`, `admin-volume-groups.js?v=17`, and `admin.js?v=124`. Released `admin.css?v=72`, `admin-volume-groups.js?v=15`, and `admin.js?v=123` stay historical. The earlier draft pin `admin-volume-groups.js?v=16` stays historical. PR #335 merged as `916973b23b91c4ec30795f98c5a43e54ee109e9e`. Its parents are `9c2b3e7ee4808f92d4f8f2c3a340041849078343` and `ff25c298c438b5df408acff8551ae3232bf3010b`. Its tree is `fd796e946576f1f2f65265db6cf6893fa67797cc`, the same tree as the reviewed head. The upload was the four changed assets `admin.html`, `admin.css`, `admin.js`, and `admin-volume-groups.js`. Bindings stayed `ASSETS` and `COVERS` (`kutadgu-covers-preview`). `KUTADGU_HOST_MODE` stayed `production` and `MULTI_VOLUME_BOOKS_ENABLED` stayed `true`. Existing production variables stayed. ## Stage 10 pin preflight

Stage 10 cancels the other shard when one shard fails, and a new commit on the same pull request cancels the older run. A file-only pin preflight runs before Chromium is installed. It requires one `shop.js` pin across HTML pages, a positive `admin.css` and `admin-volume-groups.js` pin in `admin.html`, and those asset files on disk. End-to-end assertions of the current pin read that pin from the HTML. An assertion that still names an older pin fails the preflight when the HTML pin moves. A `currentPin` literal fails whether it matches the live admin pin or an older one. A fetch or route of an old URL elsewhere in the file does not excuse a fresh-page assertion of that URL. A cached, negative, or same-function injected href check stays historical. Historical cache fixtures, including admin `v=49`, `shop.js?v=187`, and the released header bytes, stay fixed. Homepage chrome reads the price token and both card families in one turn after the discovery stylesheet's last reload has loaded. Card geometry throws if the measured boxes never stay still. Browser callbacks receive the current query as an argument. Clean-URL catalogue checks use the reserved preview books `91021` and `91022` from `scripts/preview-clean-url-books.js`; the local preview server renders those ids with `applyFoundPublicBookHead` and does not read the live catalogue. Card alignment waits for loaded fonts, decoded images, and a stable box, and pauses infinite animations. The e2e gate still fails when a shard fails, is cancelled, or is missing. Branch `cursor/stage10-ci-preflight`. PR #333 merged as `c4550fc19b4224e7cd55cbadb922bcf3ed630e97`. The reviewed head stayed `c9873f4771146f796175c4956996b2c9c3943803`. Stage 10 run [40356647614](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40356647614) succeeded and was not restarted. This CI merge does not move the runtime baseline. Tested implementation `ed1ad64da69b8a6d7726b8399d0489b17b8a9628`. `node scripts/ci-asset-pin-preflight-tests.js`, `node scripts/ci-e2e-gate-tests.js`, and `node scripts/ci-resolve-e2e-target-tests.js` passed. Local Chromium on `http://127.0.0.1:4173`, retries 0, previously passed shard 1 with 571 and skipped 1, and shard 2 with 566 passed, 4 skipped, and 1 failed. That failure was the discovery stylesheet reload moving the price font by 8px. The focused rerun of the six chrome cases and the delayed-reload regression passed, and the 1.5px check stayed. The discovery settle observer watches `document`, so it still starts when `documentElement` is absent. Local Chromium, retries 0, passed that startup regression with the same six chrome cases and the delayed-reload regression. `node scripts/ci-asset-pin-preflight-tests.js` passed, including the stale `currentPin` and unrelated-fetch regressions. No shop, SQL, or Worker change. This change does not move the runtime baseline.

## Series entry accordion — released

`#volumeSeriesForm` is an accordion. The shared fields and each part are separate sections, and only one section is open. Collapsing hides the existing controls without rebuilding them, so typed values, the selected cover file, interior image files, object URLs, upload retry state, and the request id stay on the same nodes. Completion badges call `seriesEntryPayload`. The shared section is complete when that validator accepts the current shared fields together with two valid probe parts. Empty optional shared fields stay complete. An invalid optional value, such as a year outside 1000–2100, does not. A part is complete when its price and integer stock are valid and a cover file or an already uploaded cover URL is present. Empty title, ISBN, extra metadata, and interior images stay optional. An invalid entered value, such as a bad ISBN, makes that part incomplete. Save is still the one «يۈرۈشلۈكنى ساقلاش» button. A failed save opens the first section `seriesEntryPayload` names and focuses that field when it has one. A cover-only failure opens the first part that has no file and no uploaded URL. The separate «link existing books» editor is unchanged. Pins are `admin.css?v=71`, `admin-volume-groups.js?v=14`, and `shop.js?v=188`. Released `admin.css?v=70`, `admin-volume-groups.js?v=13`, and `shop.js?v=187` stay historical. A deleted published cover variant still falls back to the original. After that fallback, assigning the same original again does not put the failed variant `srcset` back and does not clear a cover that has already decoded. Branch `cursor/volume-series-accordion`. PR #332 merged as `a775847c2d87aa0fcc0e5bc507d5c9eb06531946`. Implementation `ecb5bd687341d0977b0ae5e581ca749ec99f02bd`. The reviewed head was `48f7a536329cca85074282f91485b565bb05c94b`. Stage 10 run [40320301670](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40320301670) passed and was not restarted. No SQL. Production is Worker version `54dfbe2c-a8bd-49b6-b7cb-4114dbe2974b`, deployment `2163a893-0b18-4858-8227-0b82444df4a7`.

## Public category group — released

Before PR #331, a category created in Admin stored `book_categories.admin_group`, but the public navigation placed leaves only from its static slug lists. `list_public_book_categories` does not return that stored group, so `dunyawi-meshhur-eser-2` stays out of ئەدەبىيات even though its stored group is `literature`. Stage 137 keeps the column and changes the allowed keys to the six groups the navigation already shows: `literature`, `religion`, `history`, `language`, `family`, and `knowledge`. It maps `children` and `parenting` to `family`, and `textbooks` to `language`. It does not rewrite category identity, books, volume groups, prices, stock, orders, or URLs. The public read adds `group_key` and does not add `admin_group`. A missing or unrecognized key keeps the static slug fallback. A valid `group_key` places that category once, under that group. The admin selector uses those same six labels and no longer reads the older `discoveryGroups`. The move panel keeps Preview, Confirm, and Close, and lays those three buttons in a wrapping row that stacks below 768px. The forward file is `STAGE137_CATEGORY_PUBLIC_GROUP.sql`. Its rollback restores the Stage 136 keys and the public list without `group_key`; `language`, `family`, and `knowledge` become null because they have no unique old key. `literature`, `religion`, and `history` stay. Branch `cursor/category-public-group`. Pins shipped in that release are `admin-book-categories.js?v=8`, `admin.css?v=70`, `kutadgu-category-nav.js?v=4`, and `shop.js?v=187`. The released accordion moves `admin.css` to `?v=71` and `shop.js` to `?v=188`. Released `shop.js?v=187` stays the Stage 137 pin. PR #331 merged as `04a4739fb61d41cfa3c98d1f04b9bfec17274838`. Migration `stage137_category_public_group` is applied. The rollback file was not run.

## Admin category primary groups — merged, SQL applied

New categories store one admin-only primary group on `book_categories.admin_group`. The key is `literature`, `history`, `religion`, `children`, `parenting`, or `textbooks`. The create form requires that choice and uses the Uyghur labels from the existing `discoveryGroups`. Those groups stay storefront display groups. This column is not a parent category and is not returned by `list_public_book_categories`. Existing category rows stay unassigned. Categories that appear in more than one discovery group are not guessed. Books, volume groups, prices, stock, flags, and orders are not rewritten. The admin list hides hidden and retired rows until «يوشۇرۇن ۋە ئېلىۋېتىلگەنلەرنى كۆرسەت» is used. Those rows stay in the directory. Book transfer opens under the category being managed, still previews before confirm, still expands a volume group, and still refuses a hidden or retired destination. The forward file is `STAGE136_CATEGORY_ADMIN_GROUP.sql`. Its rollback restores the Stage 132 list and create functions and drops only `admin_group`. Branch `cursor/admin-category-groups`. This commit is the review snapshot. Pins in the working tree are `admin-book-categories.js?v=7` and `admin.css?v=69`. `node scripts/stage136-isolated-run.js`, `node scripts/admin-category-group-tests.js`, and `node scripts/book-category-client-tests.js` passed. The isolated run applied the forward file twice, rolled it back, and applied it again. Local Chromium on `http://127.0.0.1:4173` passed `tests/e2e/book-categories.spec.js` (10). The existing Stage 132 assertion file also passed on its own throwaway database. PR #330 merged as `1efd134ae8f0e87472f23399b37fd5481361473d`. Migration `stage136_category_admin_group`, version `20261026044136`, is installed on project `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh`. The rollback file was not run.

## Spelling and copy — draft, not deployed

Repository-owned UI copy is corrected without changing gift, favorite, privacy, or order behavior. Shipping notes say جەزملىنىدۇ. The privacy date stays 22 سېنتەبىر 2026. The account sentence keeps its meaning and says كۆرەلىشىڭىز. The unresolved gift state says جەزملەنمىدى. Removing a favorite says ياقتۇرغانلاردىن. The two refresh toasts keep their clause and say بەتنى يېڭىلاپ. The storage toast says تور كۆرگۈچ. The privacy site noun says تور بېتى. The credit empty state says بۇ نامغا تەۋە ئاممىۋى كىتاب يوق. The credit-page description and stored credit names stay. Search placeholders in the catalog use …. JavaScript spread syntax stays. Generated meta and fallback JSON-LD descriptions join fragments that already end in a sentence mark with a space, and they leave a real `book.description` unchanged, including internal periods, initials, decimals, and ellipses. The 170-character fallback limit stays. Analytics blocks both ئېلېكترونلۇق خەت and ئېلېكتىرونلۇق خەت and still records an ordinary title search. No event is sent by the local check. The changed pins are `shop.js?v=183`, `kutadgu-book-seo.js?v=7`, and `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=6`. Released `shop.js?v=182`, `kutadgu-category-nav.js?v=3`, `category-nav.css?v=3`, `kutadgu-book-seo.js?v=6`, and `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=5` stay historical. `romanlar.summary` still reads ئەدەبى رومانلار. A read-only directory read on 2026-10-25 shows `meshhur-shexisler.label` is already مەشھۇر شەخسلەر, `romanlar.label` is رومان, and `tarikhiy-romanlar.label` is تارىخىي رومان. Those owner edits stay outside this draft. Those values come from `book_categories`. The existing AAL2 rename can change the label and the denormalized category text, and it leaves the slug in place. No function edits `summary` after creation, and the seed does nothing on a slug conflict. This draft does not add that function, an alias, or an admin workflow, and it does not write the directory. Branch `cursor/spelling-copy`. That work merged as `52326cef50c83faa3fe4368aac75aaa4efc44452` and is the deployed baseline. The pins named above remain the released pins. No SQL and no production configuration change.

## Book sitemap members — merged and deployed

`/sitemap-books.xml` is still the Worker-generated book sitemap. With multi-volume books enabled, it reads `query_public_book_catalog` and keeps that catalogue's visibility rules. A public series stays at `/volume-group/{slug}`. Each of its public member books is also listed at `/book/{id}`. A standalone public book stays at its own canonical URL. A private or invalid group is absent, and its individually public books stay as book URLs. Inactive, unapproved, and unpriced books stay out. Canonical URLs are deduplicated. `lastmod` is emitted only when the catalogue unit has a trustworthy `updated_at` or `created_at`. A series member URL omits `lastmod` because that read does not provide the member's own timestamp. The catalogue is paged at 100 rows until the reported total is reached. A short, stalled, or changed read fails the sitemap instead of publishing a partial file. Files still split at 40,000 URLs, and also before 50,000 URLs or 50 MB uncompressed. `sitemap-pages.xml`, including its `/dastanlar` item, and `robots.txt` stay unchanged. Branch `cursor/book-sitemap-members`. Draft PR #326 started at `c801141b0ae7453ad5ffbc84dc9c5e6f79b648d7`, tree `9f21594df312a8a33cda57f6816d41ca07af9a8d`, then the handoff commit `67d551ea097804e5a6b5719001533f6217f76621`, tree `0773dc1f38a044ee03081f45be45872e7d21af27`, based on `52326cef50c83faa3fe4368aac75aaa4efc44452`. `node scripts/stage7-seo-tests.js` and `node scripts/cloudflare-preview-tests.js` passed. A read-only catalogue comparison matched 576 unique URLs, 554 books and 22 groups, with 60 additions and no removals against production. Stage 10 run [40206896584](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40206896584) failed on the first head. Run [40209046493](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40209046493) was left to finish on `67d551ea` and failed the same way. It was not restarted. The 20 failures are in tests that leave `list_public_book_categories` unmocked. The volume editor looks for تارىخىي رومانلار, while the live directory now returns تارىخىي رومان for the same source. The search test selects رومانلار and a later directory whose label is رومان clears that select, because `renderSearchOptions` keeps a value only when the label or source text still matches. A delayed directory that still uses رومانلار keeps the selection. The follow-up installs that seed directory in the affected tests and does not change the sitemap, the live directory, or the category renderer. PR #326 merged as `28020ca919d7c9dd74e87b9b03265edbe87d0b80` and is the deployed baseline. Stage 10 run [40210518921](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40210518921) succeeded on `4c4eb404392b00965e738c6d18200b193050ed77` and was not restarted. No SQL and no category-directory write.

## Series volume metadata — merged and deployed

New multi-volume entry keeps the shared title, category, description, and per-part price, stock, ISBN, cover, and interior images. It also stores the supported book columns that ordinary entry already uses: authors and translators through the existing credit plan, a publisher that can be chosen or typed, publication year, page count, cover type, and book size. Those bibliographic values have one shared default and an explicit per-part override. A blank part keeps the shared value. A filled part is not replaced by the shared value. Blank optional values stay null. `language`, `publish_date`, and `href` are not production columns and are not written. Sales count, original-price correction, and pending-only print controls stay off this form. Stock status stays automatic unless a part selects an override. The public volume read now includes each member's `pages`. The selection list and that member's own book detail show it. Missing or invalid page counts are omitted, including zero. Branch `cursor/series-volume-metadata`. Implementation `c7a9cb07de3d40d4bda90e5ff873f3d5b6af1b8d`, tree `e67ab979bf55665397fbd18d2d05e48430ea86dd`. The page-count and credit recovery is `13c6f5c6ff0782de09cd780c47ec3eed8f54f907` (tree `06867cfeb5ff9b45b547e4c0c6e60af08daf40d2`). The server volume list is inserted before the description section, so a representative book whose metadata is already filled still shows each member's own page count. A failed credit link leaves the series form and its request id in place, and the warning stays on the visible group status. Retry uses that same request and does not upload the covers again. Metadata and ordinary credit checks, including duplicate authors or translators, run before any cover upload. Pins for this draft are `shop.js?v=184`, `kutadgu-volume-storefront.js?v=4`, `admin.js?v=123`, and `admin-volume-groups.js?v=13`. Released `admin-volume-groups.js?v=12` stays historical. Released `shop.js?v=183` and `kutadgu-volume-storefront.js?v=3` stay historical. Manual SQL is `STAGE134_SERIES_VOLUME_METADATA.sql`. Its rollback restores the Stage 133 creation function and was not run. Migration `stage134_series_volume_metadata`, version `20261025192011`, is installed on project `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh`. PR #327 merged as `27b3a6aae33fbeda723207eb79158d7d578a4f13` and is the deployed baseline. The pins that shipped are `shop.js?v=184`, `kutadgu-volume-storefront.js?v=4`, `admin.js?v=123`, and `admin-volume-groups.js?v=13`. Stage 10 run [40222821471](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40222821471) succeeded on `043e96f02164b0646b3bbd80709591b7b1d9e261` and was not restarted. No production configuration change.

## Cover delivery variants — draft, not deployed

A completed derivative is advertised only when the covers bucket has a verified availability record for that source. Admin sources use `book-covers/variants/v1/availability.json`. A staff source uses `book-covers/staff/{uid}/variants/v1/availability.json`, and a staff token still cannot write `book-covers/variants/`. The static availability files stay empty, so this draft does not activate existing covers. A cold server reads the bucket object, and a signed-out browser reads the same document from its own origin. Both may keep it for 60 seconds. After that window the next read sees a newer record. That read has a 400 millisecond budget for every required document, including the object body. If it stalls, fails, or is malformed, the book or group page still returns with the last validated record or the original cover. Publication uses a create-if-absent guard and a conditional update. A null conditional put is a conflict: the writer rereads, merges, and retries until its budget ends, then reports unpublished instead of dropping another source. A failed or timed-out publication leaves the master saved and the cover on its original. An unknown cover keeps the original `src` and has no `srcset`. Detail markup also offers the original at its real width, so a 480-only cover is not upscaled when the detail slot needs more than 480 pixels. Cards continue to use the smaller verified candidate. The stored original, its `image_url`, and the 1600-pixel q0.92 master stay. Cloudflare Image Resizing is not used. Variant keys keep the source path and extension. Derivative work stays inside the eight-second master budget and does not decide the master result. GIF, SVG, animated WebP, and APNG stay on the original. Zoom and social metadata stay on the original. The changed pins are `shop.js?v=186`, `kutadgu-cover-image.js?v=5`, `kutadgu-cover-variants.js?v=5`, and `kutadgu-cover-availability.js?v=1`. Released `shop.js?v=184` and `kutadgu-cover-image.js?v=2` stay in production until this draft is deployed. `node scripts/cover-variant-generate.js` reads the public catalogue and every public series member through the existing catalogue, sitemap, and group-storefront reads. It refuses a short, stalled, or changed read, writes local files and `artifacts/cover-variants/generated-manifest.json`, and refuses `--upload`. A read on 2026-10-25 found 516 catalogue units and 23 groups, which deduplicated to 556 private covers. It recorded 699 local derivative files: 391 sources had a 480w file only, 154 had both widths, and 11 were narrower than 480w. No animated source was flattened. Each recorded file has its dimensions, byte size, and SHA-256. The deleted-variant browser check serves `/book/311` from the public book renderer and holds the catalogue read until the cold image is still present. After that read, the settled page keeps the original cover. Dark homepage color checks wait until the theme and discovery stylesheets have loaded and the body color transition matches the dark tokens. This draft does not move the runtime baseline.

## UKIJ font loading — merged and deployed

`UKIJCJK.woff2` and `UKIJCJK.ttf` stay at the same URLs and the same bytes. The primary derivatives are built from those files and keep every original codepoint except Unicode scripts Hani, Hang, Kana, Hira, and Bopo. The primary WOFF2 is 49,720 bytes and the primary TTF is 181,736 bytes. The original files are 1,172,200 and 6,089,292 bytes and both expose 30,023 codepoints. The complementary unicode-range is 10 ranges and 118 characters. Intersected with the original cmap it is still the 28,419 deferred codepoints, the primary range is still the 1,604 primary codepoints, and the mapped sets do not overlap. The range contains 16,322 unmapped holes. Ordinary UI symbols and emoji are outside it. `theme.css` is 16,917 bytes and its local Brotli size is 3,555 bytes, down from 57,037 and 11,474 with the expanded list. Ordinary Uyghur pages preload and request `UKIJCJK-primary.woff2`. A visible Chinese, Japanese, Korean, or Bopomofo character requests the original `UKIJCJK.woff2`. Family name, font-display, and each existing weight descriptor stay as they were. The changed pins are `theme.css?v=13`, `index.css?v=23`, `admin.css?v=68`, `account.css?v=9`, and `book-staff.css?v=10`. The historical admin `v=49` fixture stays. An immediate geometry sample is not stable: one pass reported four book-shell title differences at 1280px, all a 1px `y` shift with the same size, line height, and family. Waiting for fonts and for the unrounded box to repeat matched all 112 comparisons. Branch `cursor/ukij-primary-font` merged as `007a635ff101e9582da312273539a6b8e4c15449` and is the deployed runtime baseline. It does not claim an LCP change. Playwright Firefox and WebKit on Windows used the primary file and changed the sample width; that is not a physical iPhone or Safari test. The cart quantity retry was not changed here.

`admin_create_book_volume_series(uuid,jsonb)` failed in production with SQLSTATE 42703 at the book INSERT before the migration below. The old function named `href`, `language`, and `publish_date`, and then updated `href`. Production `public.books` has none of those columns. `publish_year` is a nullable integer, and the old INSERT supplied an empty string. `isbn` is NOT NULL text and an empty value stays `''`.

`STAGE133_BOOK_VOLUME_SERIES_CREATE_SCHEMA.sql` replaces only that function. It does not add the missing columns. Storefront addresses stay `/book/{id}`. Optional `publish_year` and `pages` are null when omitted or blank, and an out-of-range value is rejected before any row is kept. AAL2, the private `series_draft` status, idempotency, and the single transaction stay. The isolated check runs the function on a schema with those production column types. It passed two-part creation, fractional prices, blank optional integers, replay without duplicate books, failure rollback, and the member ids used for interior images. A browser check kept the typed draft and selected files after SQLSTATE 42703, discarded only the unreferenced cover upload, and retried interiors after the next successful response. A lost HTTP 502 response still keeps the same request and does not discard covers. PR #321 merged at `0dea686cada5337f03729c260a4567daac9cf8c9`. The function commit is `94f9b318214d9d771772f775fdbba035ec72c80f`. The installed body matches the reviewed SQL. The rollback file was not run. A Worker deployment does not install this function.

The original `/kutadgu-logo.png` and `/kutadgu-logo.webp` stay at their URLs, with the same bytes, square shape, colors, and transparency. Header and other small logo consumers use 128px WebP and PNG derivatives. The header is drawn at 38px on desktop and 32px on a narrow screen, and the shared logo class is 48px outside that override, so 128px covers those sizes on a dense display. `/favicon.ico` contains 16, 32, and 48px images. A 32px PNG favicon, a 180px Apple touch icon, and 192px and 512px PNG manifest icons declare those sizes. None is marked maskable. Open Graph, Twitter, and the store JSON-LD logo stay on the original PNG. `hero-brand-logo.png` and `hero-brand-logo.webp` stay because gallery fixtures still use them. ## Visit check in page flow — released PR #319

The visit check is a normal-flow region, shown only when Turnstile reports that a person must interact or when a failed proof or count needs an explicit retry. It is not a fixed overlay, and it does not cover the header or the mobile navigation. `appearance` is `interaction-only` and `size` is the documented normal 300 by 65 widget. The open card keeps its border and vertical padding, and leaves the widget slot the full content width so that widget stays inside the slot and the viewport at 320px. Automatic Turnstile retry is off, and expiry, timeout, network loss, and rejection use one retry button with at most three clicks. A callback is accepted only while its widget generation is still the active flow. Completion, disposal, and the third failed retry ignore later callbacks, so a finished check is not rebuilt. Completion removes the region. The same event id is kept. A second callback or an in-flight proof does not start another flow. A token is not sent again after proof acceptance. A lost count response is retried with that same id, so a committed visit is not inserted again. Dismissal and errors do not count. `analyticsHooks: false`, the production host check, and the localhost exercise attribute stay. The changed pins are `visit-proof.js?v=4` and `analytics.js?v=12`. Historical `analytics.js?v=8` cache coverage stays. These checks use mocked Turnstile and mocked RPC fixtures. They are not a live Siteverify call and they do not change the earlier LCP measurements. Chromium checks are not a physical phone or Safari.

## Multi-volume editor — released PR #313

Branch `cursor/volume-editor-drafts` keeps a published group in the list and leaves the editor ready for a new group only after publication is confirmed. A saved draft stays open so it can still be published. A failed, held, or late save, publication, or lookup does not clear or overwrite a draft opened in the meantime. Switching between the existing-book form and the new-series form invalidates that in-flight editor operation. Its completion can refresh the saved-group list, and it does not hide, clear, or replace the form the operator is using, including a typed series title, selected cover, or frozen series-save request. Clearing the editor does not unpublish, detach, or delete the saved group.

The first accepted book in a new untouched group suggests the group title and fills author, category, and description when those fields have not been edited. Later parts and an open saved group do not overwrite operator edits, including a field the operator cleared. Category and description come from the existing admin book read after the eligibility preview. A hidden category is not assigned to a new group. Part price, stock, cover, ISBN, URL, and order stay on the part. A part price does not become the full-set price.

An existing member is detached with «يۈرۈشلۈكتىن ئايرىش», and the book stays in the catalog until the operator saves. A published group asks for confirmation before that local removal and can abandon unsaved edits. A duplicate slug is explained in Uyghur. «شۇ يۈرۈشلۈكنى ئېچىش» appears only after the detail read returns the same id and slug as the listed candidate. A denied, empty, or mismatched detail keeps the draft and does not offer that action. The editor does not invent a suffixed link, reattach books, or treat a missing public card as deletion. The pins shipped with PR #313 are `admin.css?v=66` and `admin-volume-groups.js?v=10`. The warm admin stylesheet assertion still expects the active `admin.css` pin. Its historical `v=49` fixture and hash stay. PR #313 merged through the normal merge path. The reviewed head stayed `6d8cd66abd4ca21bacf93b2b783d592ea9ddb2ed`. It was not restarted. 1171 passed, 5 skipped, and 1 flaky. The flaky test was the desktop storefront-card author-size assertion; it passed on retry. Its tree is `e4ae7574fa1217775a8f94326558c78d0bd5ea04`. Production Worker version `068dbc94-d322-44d9-afac-154ad3a91944` serves that merge. ## Simpler category manager — released PR #312

Branch `cursor/simpler-category-manager` keeps the Stage 132 operations and simplifies the admin screen. The list shows the category name, book count, and visibility. One «تۈر قوشۇش» button opens name-only creation. A description stays optional. One «باشقۇرۇش» action on each open category reveals rename, reorder, hide or restore, transfer, and retirement. Raw slugs stay out of that view. Existing category URLs do not change.

Creation generates one stable slug from the name and clears the form only after confirmed success. A validation error keeps the entered name and description. The original operation id, slug, and complete payload are frozen before the create request is awaited, and the name, description, and save button stay disabled while that request is in flight. A repeated save during the request is ignored, including when the operation is busy, and does not replace the frozen payload. If the response is then lost, that same snapshot is kept across an edited description and across closing and reopening the form. A replacement slug is generated only after a definitive slug collision. Retirement confirmation names the selected category, its book and group counts, and the replacement category, and explains that the old link is preserved. An occupied category is still refused. Book transfer uses «كىتابلارنىڭ تۈرىنى ئۆزگەرتىش», opens only when requested, preselects the category that launched it, and loads that category’s books. The operator chooses the destination. Clearing the source or closing the transfer advances the book-load generation, so a late response cannot repaint the previous books. Atomic moves, volume-group expansion, hidden and retired destination checks, stale-response protection, and frozen retries stay. Category-manager labels, book titles, and retirement buttons stay at or above 4.5:1 in both themes. A book checkbox stays a normal control beside its title, and the row is the click target.

Homepage category tests install a directory response before navigation. A fresh session whose directory is unavailable keeps the static cards and their legacy placement. A successful directory replaces those cards with the fixture’s visible links, labels, and order, including the literature hub, and leaves hidden and retired rows out. A delayed response is asserted only after it arrives. Managed cards flow in regular rows as the count changes. The static fallback keeps its existing placement. `index.html` requests `home-category-appended-cards.css?v=3`. Local Chromium on that head passed `tests/e2e/homepage-compact.spec.js` with retries disabled, including the delayed directory, and `tests/e2e/book-categories.spec.js`. The My Books cart-alignment check waits until the injected alignment and card-polish stylesheets have applied, fonts are loaded, and cover boxes have height, then still requires cart bottoms within 2px.

No SQL was added. The pins shipped with PR #312 are `admin.css?v=65`, `admin-book-categories.js?v=6`, and `home-category-appended-cards.css?v=3`. The warm admin stylesheet assertion expected that active `admin.css` pin. Its historical `v=49` fixture and hash stayed. PR #312 merged through the normal merge path. The reviewed head stayed `0195a7a026805a610d0fe07a89a5b766beb6356f`. Stage 10 run [40024347100](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/40024347100) succeeded on that head and was reused. It was not restarted. 1164 passed, 5 skipped, 1 flaky, and 0 failed. The flaky test was the signed-out book-review invitation; it passed on retry. The merge commit is `a20185a4176ba8779459992f3fe399a1088d7b9f`. Its parents are `6cfc2b1fec6247478b6d859f68f667a2e58cb57f` and `0195a7a026805a610d0fe07a89a5b766beb6356f`. Its tree is `6aa9452b09932f1aec39b52e1c813b64f6382850`. Production Worker version `8f4ff372-592e-4d76-a35b-4b525ecb2eb0` serves that merge. ## Admin-managed book categories — released PR #311

Branch `cursor/admin-book-categories` adds a directory of book categories. The association stays the existing `source` value, such as `romanlar.html`. A displayed name can change without changing that source, a book ID, a book or group URL, a price, stock, a cover, publication, or an order. `adabiyat` stays a literature hub and is not a book source. The 18 existing categories are seeded once. The seed does not rewrite books or volume groups, and it does not move an unknown source into Universal.

`STAGE132_BOOK_CATEGORIES.sql` is additive and manual. It creates `book_categories` and `book_category_operations`, enables row-level security, and grants the public read to `anon` and `authenticated`. Admin functions stay executable only by `authenticated` and still require `is_kutadgu_admin()` plus AAL2. A request id makes a completed admin operation return its stored result on retry. A rename updates the directory label and the denormalized label on books and groups with that source. Ordering swaps two visible positions. Hiding removes a category from discovery and from new-selection lists. It does not unpublish books. Deletion is a retirement: it is refused while any book or group still uses the source, including inactive, hidden, pending, and draft rows, and the old slug remains as a redirect. A retired slug cannot be created again. A bulk move can empty any category, including Universal as a destination, but not a hidden or retired destination. It expands every member of a touched volume group, locks books, members, and groups before the destination, and refuses the write when the expanded set changed. The preview lists the original categories of the selected books, the extra books and groups pulled in by expansion, and inactive, pending, hidden, and draft rows. One failed statement rolls the move back. No category operation deletes a book.

Ordinary book and group writes cannot attach a retired source. `book_categories_guard_source()` locks the matching category row before an insert or an update of `source` or `category`, including when the source value is unchanged, and copies that row's label onto the book or group. A deferred constraint checks retirement again at commit. A source that is not in the directory, including a legacy unknown source, stays allowed and keeps the submitted label. Title, price, stock, cover, and URL are otherwise left as the writer sent them. The same trigger covers ordinary saves, quick edits, new entries, staff saves, and volume-group writes. Rename, retirement, and membership changes lock the affected book and group rows before the category row. `list_public_book_categories` returns `categories` for discovery and `direct` for every non-retired category, including hidden ones. A directory failure is not treated as proof that a category is absent.

Until `list_public_book_categories` exists, storefront and admin pages stay on the hardcoded category list. After a successful read, that response is authoritative and is cached for the browser session. A later failed read keeps the last authoritative list. A browser that has never received the directory leaves the static homepage cards in place. The Worker renders both existing and new category documents with the current label, summary, canonical URL, Open Graph and Twitter fields, heading, intro, current navigation label, structured data, and `data-catalog-source`. New URLs use `universal.html` as the template and do not need a new static file. Hidden categories stay out of discovery, search, sitemap, and new-selection lists, and their direct category and book links still resolve. A category-shaped URL returns 503 while the directory cannot be read. It returns 404 only after a successful directory omits that slug. An existing static category file still renders during a directory failure. A known retired category returns 308 to its replacement. `/sitemap-pages.xml` is in `assets.run_worker_first`, so the Worker appends managed categories to the served sitemap.

The pins shipped with PR #311 are `admin.css?v=63`, `admin.js?v=122`, `admin-volume-groups.js?v=8`, `book-staff.js?v=12`, `shop.js?v=178`, and `premium-ux.js?v=21`. New scripts are `kutadgu-book-categories.js?v=1` and `admin-book-categories.js?v=3`. The warm admin stylesheet assertion reads the current `admin.html` pin. On this Windows checkout that test still stops at its fixture blob hash before the pin assertion.

## Existing-book lookup for volume groups — released PR #310

Branch `cursor/volume-group-book-lookup` extends «بۇرۇن قوشقان كىتابتىن قىسىم قوشۇش». A numeric book ID still uses `admin_preview_book_volume_members` alone, and an ISBN-shaped value of at most 32 characters still uses that preview. A title or author query, including text longer than 32 characters, goes only to the existing admin `searchOrFilter` on `books`. It uses the signed-in admin's permissions and walks every match 40 rows at a time. It does not reuse the current admin list page or that page's filters. Results show the cover, title, author, price, ISBN when the book has one, the internal ID, and «قىسىم قىلىپ قوشۇش». Choosing a catalog row runs the same preview before the existing record is appended. The book ID, URL, price, stock, cover, and stored title stay on that record. A duplicate in the unsaved list, or a book already linked to another group, is refused. Similar titles do not form a group or choose part order. Labels stay manual. An older search cannot replace a newer result. Starting a new draft, opening another group, or leaving the editor drops a pending search, a pending result page, and a pending candidate preview, so a late selection is not added to the other draft. A failed search can be retried. The two-part minimum, 100-part cap, separate publication, and frozen new-series save stay as they are.

## Missing set prices — released PR #309

Branch `cursor/missing-set-price-phase2` keeps a missing multi-volume price unavailable. Null, undefined, a blank or whitespace string, and an invalid or non-finite value stay unavailable through normalization. A real numeric zero and a valid numeric string stay prices. A valid explicit set price wins, including 750 and zero. Otherwise the non-quote fallback sums member prices only when every member price is valid and, when a member count is present, the list is that long. An empty or incomplete group does not become zero or a partial total.

Without an authoritative quote, the affected cart row and the aggregate total show «باھا تېخى بېكىتىلمىگەن». A mixed cart does not present the known book subtotal as its complete total. Removing the unknown set restores that known total. Quote pending stays «جەزملەۋاتىدۇ…». A quote or group read error stays «—» and keeps retry. An authoritative quote, including 750, still settles the row and the total and can open checkout. A valid zero stays available and displays 0 ₺. Missing member prices keep the set unavailable and checkout closed. A confirmed stock block stays a stock block. Cart keys stay `kutadgu-cart-v1` and `kutadgu-set-cart-v1`.

The detail fallback uses the same rule. series-156 keeps the explicit 750 beside part prices 370 and 399. A missing detail set price uses «باھا تېخى بېكىتىلمىگەن», not a partial sum. The released #306 note that `groupSetPrice` treats a null member price as zero describes the behavior this correction replaces.

Changed runtime pins shipped as `shop.js?v=176`, `kutadgu-volume-groups.js?v=2`, and `kutadgu-volume-storefront.js?v=3`. `premium-ux.js?v=19` and `shop.css?v=58` stayed. ## Series part removal — released PR #307

Branch `cursor/series-part-remove-306` makes an accidentally added series-entry row removable. Each part heading has `× بۇ قىسىمنى ئېلىۋېتىش`, still wired to `[data-series-remove]` and `removeSeriesPart()`. The accessible name includes the current part number. The button is at least 44px, keyboard reachable, and keeps a visible focus outline. Two rows stay, and the form says «ئاز دېگەندە ئىككى قىسىم قېلىدۇ». Removing a row updates the count, numbers, move buttons, and add button immediately. Typed values, covers, interior files, and image order stay on the surviving rows. If the representative row goes, the first remaining row becomes the representative. This only removes an unsaved form row. Saved books, remote images, the 100-part cap, four interior images, separate save and publication, and the frozen request after an uncertain save stay as they are.

`admin.html` requests `admin.css?v=61` and `admin-volume-groups.js?v=6` at that release. `admin.js?v=121` stays. The application commit is `1875daac9712e7c0bc1f1c0a39a5887f61fa99b8`. PR #307 merged at 2026-10-22T11:59:17Z. The merge commit is `70013f9bb2245743cdf0454465c15a4cd9b84c24`. Its parents are `92ef4856f452f191631dd7d8b38af9131243554c` and `f9071fbc755ccd79fa439ed66accadc03872dcf9`. Its tree is `d015112e6309aa53845614171648e519de882543`, the same as `f9071fbc755ccd79fa439ed66accadc03872dcf9`. That merge was the runtime baseline until PR #309. This session did not repeat its Worker deploy and does not record a new version id.

Local checks on 2026-10-22, Chromium only, not a physical phone or iPad. `node scripts/multi-volume-series-entry-tests.js` passed. Isolated admin fixtures with write guards passed the new removal test, the 2-to-100 removal case, the new-series save, the failed-save retry, and the lost-response retry that still sends the frozen payload. The removal test clicked through 2→3 by keyboard, then 2→4→3→2, checked the two-row minimum, the saved prices and titles, surviving covers and interior order, and representative reassignment. RTL had no horizontal overflow at 1280×900 and 390×844. Screenshots are in `artifacts/series-part-remove/`. On this Windows checkout the warm admin-CSS test stops at the fixture blob hash before the pin assertion; that file was not edited for the series-part release, and the assertion expected `admin.css?v=61` at that release.

## Compact grouped storefront cards — draft notes, superseded by the release above

Branch `cursor/compact-series-cards-40af` compacts the multi-volume cards that already appear. A grouped card shows the stored title, an em dash, and the part count, then the author, one full-set price, and one «تەپسىلاتى» link. The count comes from `memberCount` or `member_count` when that is a positive integer, otherwise from member ids, otherwise from member prices. Stored titles are not rewritten. The composed title stays in the element and on its `title` attribute when the visible line is clamped. Separate part-count badges, individual part prices, price explanations, and extra group actions are removed from those cards.

An explicit `completeSetPrice` or `complete_set_price` wins, including a real zero. series-156 cards show 750 TRY, not the 370 + 399 sum of 769 and not the starting part price. With no explicit price, the card uses `effectiveSetPrice` or `effective_set_price`. Member prices are summed only when the list is the same length as the expected part count and every entry is a finite number, including zero. Null, empty, and whitespace entries stay missing. A short list is not treated as complete. Otherwise the card shows «باھا تېخى بېكىتىلمىگەن». The same rule is in shop cards, the premium fallback, and category SSR. Premium discovery does not gain new catalog rows. Detail pages keep part selection, the individual prices, the explicit-set explanation, and the full-set purchase control. Group URLs, direct child URLs, grouping before pagination, and the authoritative cart quote stay as they are. The non-quote `groupSetPrice` fallback still treats a null member price as zero; the cart path was not changed. Sales modes are not in this change.

Storefront documents request `shop.js?v=175`. `shop.js` requests `premium-ux.js?v=19`, `kutadgu-volume-storefront.js?v=2`, and `storefront-cards-1a-polish.css?v=4`. `shop.css?v=58`, `listing-card-safety.css?v=2`, and `premium-ux.css?v=10` stay. Admin pins stay `admin.css?v=60`, `admin-volume-groups.js?v=5`, and `admin.js?v=121`. Carousel titles keep the existing two-line reserve with `min-height` important so a one-line ordinary title and a wrapped series title share the same action row.

This draft did not move the runtime baseline, which was then `e1a42046794ee71fee6b56415b6f8dbf61d0fc6c`. PR #304 and PR #305 stay released. No SQL, production save, merge, deploy, flag change, admin-form change, or category-management change was made.

Local checks on 2026-10-22 at 11:36 Europe/Istanbul. They are not a Stage 10 run and they did not touch production. The price and fixture correction is `d0506cfa3bd20178a12f670224c51308c14737d6`. The tested head is the commit that records this SHA. Chromium only, at 1280×900, 768×1024, and 390×844, in light and dark. No physical phone or iPad was used.

`node scripts/compact-series-card-tests.js`, `multi-volume-storefront-contract-tests.js`, `books-visit-rotation-tests.js`, and `web-app-manifest-tests.js` passed. The new card test runs `normalizeCatalogItem`, `normalizeCatalogBook`, and `normalizeCategoryRow` into the real card markup for a complete sum, a partial list, an all-missing list, blank values, and a valid zero. `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1 KUTADGU_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173 npx playwright test tests/e2e/compact-series-cards.spec.js --project=chromium` passed. Catalog, group, quote, and detail reads are stubbed locally, and an unexpected external request fails the test. The spec does not inject card, grid, or carousel geometry. The same local server then passed the four `multi-volume storefront` tests and the five isolated `set-cart quote settlement` tests. The live `KUTADGU_LIVE_SET_CART` test stayed skipped. The detail fixture still shows 750 beside part prices 370 and 399. The isolated cart still quotes 750 ₺.

Refreshed screenshots are in `/opt/cursor/artifacts/compact-series-cards/`. The six `desktop|tablet|mobile` × `light|dark` files are the books listing. The matching `*-carousel.png` files are the homepage carousel. The earlier shots used a synthetic host with a custom 3/2/1 grid and forced the carousel into a two-column grid at 430px. These use the production listing grid: three columns at 1280 and two columns at 768 and 390. The mobile carousel is one full-width card. Covers and actions line up in both themes. The missing-price card shows «باھا تېخى بېكىتىلمىگەن», and the complete three-part card shows 360.

`scripts/storefront-cards-1a-polish-tests.js`, `storefront-cards-1b-polish-tests.js`, and `public-book-card-row-alignment-tests.js` fail on this Windows checkout before their product assertions: `git diff` is invoked with a trailing semicolon, and the tracked files use LF while the worktree uses CRLF. Branch `cursor/catalog-cover-reliability` repairs storefront first paint and cover recovery. The repair commit is `97508d5b19014ebde0c1ca54a4b0b2511943eb54`. The approved head is `7236044e7fc7adcb13eddf53e2bc297822242293`. Storefront documents request `shop.js?v=173`, and `shop.js` requests `premium-ux.js?v=17`. Production pages also request `/kutadgu-image-storage.js`.

On the released code, a controlled local trace showed the homepage catalog RPC had not started while the availability HEAD was still pending, and a failed book read replaced a server-rendered detail cover with the unavailable placeholder. A preview image whose source changed from book A to book B kept A's stored origin, so an error could restore A. Same-key refresh failures replaced featured, carousel, search, listing, and discovery results with the error state.

The repair starts the page and catalog reads without waiting for that optional HEAD, and a late probe failure does not clear a catalog that has already loaded. A metadata failure keeps a trustworthy server-rendered or already decoded detail cover and does not add a purchase control. Confirmed hidden or empty book reads still remove the cover. A failed page read stays unavailable for a numeric book or when remote product authority is required. On a local fixture where that authority is off, the same failed read leaves authority idle so the existing static page can still paint. It does not restore a hidden book or a purchase from stale price or stock. Stage 10 run [39694462819](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/39694462819) on `e47971f7b4cbd822a608fbe1fe9529e47b992c5d` failed those two availability checks and the local static fixture. Run [39697359206](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/39697359206) on `a34fdeb0c119293564f35ab97874eda5321a0c5c` then failed because the discovery loading sentence was measured above a reserved grid taller than the viewport. Stage 10 run [39700051186](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/39700051186) succeeded on `bbec1ac349153cf8efbe8a018fd51e88a03457f0` and is not rerun for this head. Review of that commit found three local failures: a gallery choice did not cancel an already queued hero recovery, so the old cover replaced the new one when the timer fired; keeping a decoded cover returned before title, credit, and description updates; a failed load-more hid the control after re-enabling it. The follow-up invalidates that recovery by generation and source, continues the metadata update, and leaves the same next-page control usable. One recovery attempt and hidden-book removal stay. The approved head is `7236044e7fc7adcb13eddf53e2bc297822242293`. If the hero image is replaced after a load error, one later attempt restores it, and a gallery choice can restore it as well. A changed cover source replaces the stored origin, and a late error whose assigned source is no longer current does not restore another book. A failed refresh keeps matching ready or empty content; a first load with nothing to keep, a changed query, and a confirmed hidden book stay distinct.

Homepage featured, carousel, and discovery cards are not server-rendered, so the first titles still wait for the catalog read. Category listings and detail pages already had server-rendered content to keep. Desktop Chromium and desktop WebKit, including viewport sizes used in the fixture, are not a physical iPad. Local Chromium first featured text during a hanging HEAD was 1096 ms, with the catalog RPC at 1020 ms. Local WebKit was 1850 ms and 1799 ms. Cold homepage text was 767 ms in Chromium and 872 ms in WebKit; the warm reload was 344 ms and 736 ms. After this deploy, a write-guarded Chromium check of `https://www.kutadgubilik.com`, TLS verification left on, used a certificate for `kutadgubilik.com` issued by Google Trust Services, with SAN `kutadgubilik.com`, `www.kutadgubilik.com`, and `*.www.kutadgubilik.com`. Live `shop.js?v=173` is 329677 bytes, SHA-256 `9832de56536b66e7b72b403186862a6acab32b51e59f3653ee2934eeac96ca62`. Live `premium-ux.js?v=17` is 30423 bytes, SHA-256 `55b8e27632f4e10a85526705fb04ecffb6feb681ff9338a92fcaccfaa43c2258`. Live `kutadgu-image-storage.js` is 19043 bytes, SHA-256 `6b1efd5cf95e16065aadb6e577e62468ab91e577c7383fd14973944465b6b4d5`. All three match the catalog merge. Served pages request `shop.js?v=173` and `/kutadgu-image-storage.js`; `shop.js` then requests `premium-ux.js?v=17`. `/romanlar.html` returns 308 to `/romanlar`. Featured text appeared at 739 ms and, on a second pass, 1248 ms. The page showed 20 featured titles, 15 carousel titles, and, after a discovery group click, 8 discovery cards. Search for زېمىن opened `/volume-group/series-156`. Romanlar load-more grew from 24 cards to 48 with no duplicate links. `/book/569` decoded its cover, a gallery click changed the hero to a different `/__r2/` gallery object, reload kept a decoded cover, and Back returned to `/romanlar` with a decoded card image. The existing series «ئويغانغان زېمىن» stored set `group_id` 1 qty 1 and left the ordinary cart empty. The cart row and summary showed **750 ₺** with quantity 1 and no quantity click. Checkout was not opened. Public read and quote RPCs were allowed. Visit, analytics, RUM, and `analytics_events` posts were aborted. No order or admin save was sent. Homepage featured, carousel, and discovery still wait for a catalog response; discovery cards wait for a group choice as well. This is desktop Chromium, not a physical iPad. That catalog pass did not exercise the admin form; the series-form section above did. This documentation commit is not deployed.

The commit that records this paragraph does not move the runtime baseline.

The vendored Supabase client returns HTTP 500 `{"message":"Internal Server Error"}` and HTTP 502 `{"message":"Bad Gateway"}` with `error.status` unset and the status only on the RPC result. `c8b0ce24` dropped that status in `rpc()`, so both looked like ordinary failures and cover cleanup ran. The volume RPC helper now copies `result.status` onto the error. Cleanup runs only when that status is a completed 4xx other than 408 or 429 and the error code is a PostgreSQL SQLSTATE, which is the positive sign that the function raised and the transaction rolled back. Anything else, including these gateway bodies, stays retryable. Chromium on `d0418bd72a8968a66f2e9c07918f4d2d1422827c` drove both responses through `window.supabase` 2.45.4: each kept the request id, payload, and cover URLs, uploaded two covers rather than four, deleted none, and the next save recovered the series without another upload. A returned `P0001` at HTTP 400 still deleted only the uncommitted series covers. No production SQL was applied and no flag was turned on. The runtime baseline stays `734c18cf232e6c49cf80f98390147cf6300f7d1c`.

A group links existing books. Each volume keeps its own `books` row, id, cover, price, stock, URL, and history. A group may contain 2 to 100 volumes. The public catalog counts one group before pagination, search, sorting, and filtering. Price sorting and filtering use the lowest available member price. An explicit `complete_set_price` is the set price. A null set price is the current sum of the member prices. Quotes last five minutes and carry a fingerprint. Order snapshots, stock deduction, cancellation and restoration, and deletion protection expand a set into its component volumes. Shared-cart v2 can carry a typed set line. Shared-cart v1 and a flat order stay readable. Copying an order still accepts blank customer details and does not save history. WhatsApp still requires those details. A signed-in WhatsApp order reserves `about:blank` on the click, writes member history before navigating that window, and closes it when the quote is stale, expired, or out of stock.

The reviewed `app-config.js` keeps `multiVolumeBooks:false`. Group routes, grouped category pages, grouped sitemaps, and grouped AI results also require `MULTI_VOLUME_BOOKS_ENABLED=true`. Both stay off; the released Worker now serves the reviewed config with that key set to false. The reviewed apply order is in `MULTI_VOLUME_BOOKS_RELEASE.md`. Those four files are now recorded by the migration names above. Do not apply them again. Do not remove the component-aware stock helpers while a set order exists.

These runs do not include the following boundary correction. Isolated PostgreSQL for Stages 127–130 was not rerun in that earlier pass.

### Feature-on catalog filter boundary correction

A subsequent review reproduced two independent defects using the actual JavaScript query serializer and isolated PostgreSQL: the browser sends `sources:null` and `ids:null`, which Stage 130 treated as invalid arrays; and absent/blank price bounds were serialized as zero, silently filtering all positive-priced books. The old browser tests mocked RPC responses, and the old SQL checks omitted those optional keys, so the successful Stage 10 runs above did not exercise that boundary.

`catalogQuery` now preserves absent/blank price bounds as null while retaining an explicit numeric or string zero. `query_public_book_catalog` normalizes JSON-null array options to SQL NULL before validation. Explicit arrays, empty-array semantics, scalar rejection, typed IDs, and real price filters stay enforced. That corrected Stage 130 text is the text recorded in `stage130_book_volume_storefront`, `stage130_book_volume_storefront_b`, and `stage130_book_volume_storefront_c`.

Focused validation: the new JavaScript assertion failed before the fix (`0 !== null`) and passes afterward; the storefront, typed-cart and visit-order node contract suites pass. Nine actual JavaScript-query-to-PostgreSQL cases pass, and the entire Stage 130 SQL assertion file passes including the new browser-shaped null/filter cases. This SQL validation used isolated PGlite PostgreSQL 18.6, not production PostgreSQL 17.6, not the multi-session shell runner, and not a physical browser/device. The original checkout and production state were untouched. The corrected head requires its own Stage 10 result; reuse the automatic PR run rather than starting a duplicate. Public exposure remains off.

Chromium at 390×844 opened the live homepage. The recently added section reached `data-catalog-state="ready"` and showed `سۆيگۈم ۋە غورۇرۇم`. Searching that title returned `data-search-state="results"` and the same title. Setting the minimum price to 10 showed the chip `ئەڭ تۆۋەن باھا: 10 ×`. Reset cleared the input, returned the results to `idle`, and left `.premium-active-filters` with no chip. Every non-GET request was aborted in the browser before it left the test, including `POST /api/analytics-event`, two `POST /rest/v1/analytics_events` attempts, `POST /api/visit-count`, and `POST /cdn-cgi/rum`. No production analytics row was written by this check. This is Chromium emulation, not the customer’s physical phone. No order was submitted and no catalog row was written.

The proven client bug is that a failed homepage search and a confirmed empty search both used `.search-empty`. `shop.js` painted `ئىزدەش ۋاقىتلىق ئىشلىمىدى.` on failure. `premium-ux.js` `setupSmartEmptyState()` then rewrote every `.search-empty` to `ئىزدەش نەتىجىسى تېپىلمىدى`. That rewrite does not depend on geography, a Workers quota, or a Cloudflare block. The draft gives `#searchResults` `data-search-state` values `loading`, `failed`, `empty`, `results`, and `idle`. Only `empty`, after a successful zero-row response, receives the smart empty actions. A failure uses `.search-failed`, keeps the failure sentence, and offers `قايتا سىناش`. The same query, category, collection, prices, and sort are kept. One automatic retry waits inside the same request token and then stops. A failure does not emit `search` or `zero_result_search`.

A query or price edit invalidates that token immediately, before the 400ms debounce starts the next search. The generation bumps, the in-flight read is aborted, and the retry wait is aborted, so the old query is not sent again and its result is not painted under the new input. Category, collection, and sort still start the replacement search immediately. Reset clears the fields and invalidates in the same turn, leaving `idle` without another read. After those values are cleared it bubbles one `input` event, which is what `premium-ux.js` uses to rebuild `.premium-active-filters`. That event is dispatched on the reset button, so it does not schedule the debounced catalog search. A newer search also supersedes an in-flight load-more; a second load-more while one is already running still does nothing. The explicit `قايتا سىناش` button uses one `forceRecovery` read, so a prompt click during the two-second catalog recovery cooldown reaches the network. The automatic retry does not use `forceRecovery`. While the catalog is still unavailable it waits for the longer of 350ms and the remaining cooldown, and the loading state stays up through that wait. There is still only one automatic retry.

A missing book section and a missing cover are different. `markCoverUnavailable` replaces only the image with `.book-cover-unavailable`. The title stays. The homepage “recently added” section is a separate `sort=new` catalog read from a named search. After the availability HEAD has marked the remote catalog available, a failed page stays an error and does not clear that flag, so a later named search can still succeed. A successful empty page says `كىتابلار تېخى قوشۇلمىغان.` and is not the transport failure. The featured section now sets `data-catalog-state` to `loading`, `empty`, `ready`, or `failed`.

What this session did not prove: there is no customer request log here, so the first missing section could have been an HTTP failure, a confirmed empty page, a discovery-index failure, or the two-second cooldown that applies only while the catalog is still recovering. This draft does not identify which of those happened on the phone. Chromium at 390×844 is not that phone.

Storefront documents request `shop.js?v=166`. `shop.js` requests `premium-ux.js?v=16`. SHA-256 is `shop.js` `f7024b8dcb7857c9199c4e1945553b328ce849886fc84bd2afa32d7f75e1c911` (277168 bytes), `premium-ux.js` `370195a2c2bde4887f439b71f0c7e996ec8d17fc4b6fc32e40018fc9cc9ddd00` (29166 bytes), `index.html` `daf1dff95d8f85d5691c02d27d39ef529665336b5f9577a43d043b1213e289b1` (29795 bytes), and `book-shell.html` `0cdbd83574ebbbbc824f3a4a14c368364c8cc2985bbc16a1cf1d51a451ae7169` (12887 bytes).

Local evidence on `85ef258ef009dda84ad9b7e78f7823a0e5974fa8`, before this release. `node scripts/catalog-search-state-tests.js`, `scripts/homepage-discovery-authoritative-tests.js`, `scripts/web-app-manifest-tests.js`, and `scripts/guest-cart-invite-tests.js` passed. `scripts/stage-seo-2h-category-listing-seo-tests.js` still fails only its pre-existing `iqtisad` and `taamlar` checks; the frozen-file check passed. `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1 KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173 npx playwright test tests/e2e/catalog-search-failure.spec.js --reporter=line` passed 8 tests. The previous seven still pass. The new case sets the minimum price to 10, clicks reset, and checks that the input is empty, the results are `idle`, `.premium-active-filters` has no chip, and no `price=gte` catalog read starts. ## Admin activity lists — draft, not applied

Draft branch `cursor/admin-analytics-activity-e5d6`, draft PR #295. `origin/main` is the PR #294 merge `7af3fa1402b376ac30f567a6aa7ee7a5406f16e7`. This draft replaces the five simultaneous admin analytics lists with one results area. The categories stay: viewed books, books added to cart, WhatsApp order clicks, searches, and searches with no results. The default order is the latest matching event in the selected period. It is not the highest count and it is not the book's publication date. Search rows are stored phrases. A phrase is not shown as a searched book. The default scope is verification-passed browsers. `بارلىق خام خاتىرىلەر` is the raw alternative and is not relabeled. The four summary counts, the funnel, and the no-result summary card stay raw. That card reads `get_kutadgu_zero_searches` `total_events` for the selected summary period. It does not copy the activity list, including a verified or custom-date total, and it does not add up the capped page. A real zero stays 0. A failed read keeps the last total only with a notice naming that period and saying the requested period did not load. A same-period failure marks the kept number as not refreshed. A failure with no earlier total paints `—`, not 0. A missing function paints `—`. Success clears the notice. A late response for an older period does not replace the current total or notice. Verified visit cards stay visit counts. Stage 126 is manual and unapplied. This draft is not deployed, and it does not move the runtime baseline.

`STAGE126_ANALYTICS_ACTIVITY.sql` adds private action attestations, an attestation-start marker, and a separate outage signal. The Worker stores the event and its attestation in one `kutadgu_ingest_analytics_event` call. The attestation is written only when that call inserts the row, the event has an id, and the signed `kb_visit` cookie is still inside its 12-hour verification window. The visitor id comes from that cookie. A submitted visitor id or a client flag is ignored. A repeated request for an event that is already attested returns retry and leaves the stored action and visitor unchanged. A repeated request for an unverified row returns conflict and does not attach the later cookie. `kutadgu_attest_analytics_event` no longer writes. A missing or expired cookie stores the raw event and does not attest it. An ingest failure while a current cookie was sent returns 503 and notes an action outage; it does not leave an attested row. Direct anon and authenticated inserts cannot write the private table. Historical events are not backfilled. A verified window that ends at or before the marker is unavailable, not zero. A window that crosses the marker, or an attestation outage, is partial. Raw scope includes unattested rows and stays `raw_analytics_events`. Rollback is `STAGE126_ANALYTICS_ACTIVITY_ROLLBACK.sql`. It drops the ingest function and does not drop visit receipts or `analytics_events`.

A completed no-result search still emits `search` and `zero_result_search` as a pair. Inside the selected scope and time window, a confirmed zero is `search` with `result_count = 0` whenever that window contains any `search` event. The companion is not counted again, and two such searches remain two actions. If that window has no `search` event, a legacy `zero_result_search` counts when `result_count` is null or 0. A positive result count is not a zero. A `search` with a null result count stays unknown. An unattested search does not hide an attested legacy zero in the verified scope.

Dates are Europe/Istanbul calendar dates. The server query is half-open: `[start 00:00, end+1 day 00:00)`. Presets are today, yesterday, the last 7 calendar days, the last 30 calendar days, and a custom range. Grouping, sorting, the text filter, and pagination run on the full eligible set. Every filter edit, including an incomplete custom date and a query keystroke before the debounce, drops the in-flight request. A failed category, scope, or other filter change does not keep unrelated rows. Rows stay on screen only when their category, scope, sort, preset, dates, query, and offset match the request. `admin.html` requests `admin.css?v=57`, `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=15`, and `admin.js?v=118`.

Local evidence on application commit `89cb01ab9956178769319b1483e1f499df6ef704`. `bash scripts/stage126-isolated-postgres.sh` passed on throwaway PostgreSQL (`rows=56`): Istanbul day bounds, grouped latest timestamps, sort and pagination, a verified retry that keeps the first action and visitor, a conflict that does not promote an unverified `book_view`, no dangling attestation, anon insert, expired-window unavailability, partial coverage, raw scope, and rollback. The same run counts a client `search` plus `zero_result_search` pair once in both raw and verified scope, keeps two repeats as two actions, leaves an unknown result and a positive `zero_result_search` out of the zero list, counts a legacy-only null result, and keeps an attested legacy zero in verified scope when the only search in that window is unattested. `node scripts/analytics-activity-tests.js` passed for a missing cookie, an expired cookie, a verified retry, a conflict that does not call the old attest function, a second event id, and an ingest failure with a current cookie that returns 503 and notes one action outage. `node scripts/verified-visits-tests.js`, `scripts/visit-country-tests.js`, `scripts/three-hour-visits-tests.js`, `scripts/admin-book-staff-tests.js`, `scripts/cover-upload-optimize-tests.js`, and `scripts/admin-zero-searches-tests.js` passed. `scripts/admin-navigation-tests.js` still fails its existing `admin.css?v=48` pin; that pin is already absent on `origin/main`. `scripts/admin-daily-visitors-tests.js` still fails that same pin, its `{once:true}` check, and a legacy schema retry count of 3 against 2; those failures are already present on the previous head `7a245920b3506b180104d955c82dec6cb556ac8a`. `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1 KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173 npx playwright test tests/e2e/admin-analytics-activity.spec.js tests/e2e/admin-search-lists.spec.js tests/e2e/admin-zero-searches.spec.js --reporter=line` passed 4 tests. The cases cover one category, a stale book response that cannot replace a custom-date prompt, a failed sort or category change that does not keep the previous rows, an unavailable window that is not painted as zero, a missing function, a phrase that is escaped and not labeled as a book, and 390px RTL controls. Chromium is not a physical phone. No production SQL was applied, no Worker was deployed, and no analytics event was written to production. Local `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1 KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173 npx playwright test --reporter=line` on `89cb01ab9956178769319b1483e1f499df6ef704` finished 1059 passed, 2 failed, and 3 skipped. The two failures were `tests/e2e/book-reviews.spec.js` “signed-out readers see approved text and a compact invitation” (`boundingBox()` was null) and `tests/e2e/maintenance-mode.spec.js` “D authenticated Admin bypasses storefront; Admin login stays open” (the detail title was still empty).
