Those dashboard versions kept the released script etag `f09a5f6f24afb835a3ed7337f5c3457d8fff957ab4884e18842ee59ce0cfa2d6` from version `273b64d6-a92e-442f-9113-14e7abbc0a78`. `VISIT_SIGNING_SECRET` was absent, so a new 32-byte value was installed as an encrypted secret and was not printed or committed. That secret upload created Worker version `802e18ef-177f-464c-b4ce-0f92d6010857`, deployment `765b180a-7dfd-47f6-b6af-dcc60c540327`, at 2026-10-16T19:46:42.589228Z, source `wrangler`, triggered by `secret`, at 100%. The immediate rollback of that secret upload is deployment `ccf6688c-c34b-41f2-a9db-5a9ebbfe69ff`, version `6bdc6bdf-e1bb-4536-9929-ad1f2ab88624`, created 2026-10-16T19:42:30.115192Z. The same script etag is on the new version. Verified counting is not active. The paragraphs below that still call PR #293 an undeployed draft, and that still name production as the PR #292 Worker, describe that work before its release. The owner confirmed the header behaves normally after PR #293. That confirmation is not a proven rendering root cause.

The new admin metric is a verification-passed browser visit. It is not a verified person and it is not guaranteed bot-free traffic. `visit-proof.js?v=2` runs only on `kutadgubilik.com` and `www.kutadgubilik.com`, and on localhost only when the page sets `data-kutadgu-visit-exercise` for the regression. It asks `POST /api/visit-proof` to check a Turnstile token at Siteverify, then `POST /api/visit-count` only after the browser returns the signed cookie. The proof response itself does not count. After a successful count the check panel is removed. `KUTADGU_APP_CONFIG.featureFlags.analyticsHooks === false` returns before the count, the configuration request, and the Turnstile script. `analytics.js?v=10` no longer sends `page_view` at `DOMContentLoaded`. Search, cart, book, and other events still use the Worker and may fall back to direct Supabase inserts. Those raw events are not verified visits. A failed proof does not block browsing, catalog loading, search, cart, or checkout. An interactive Turnstile step shows `زىيارەت سانىنى دەلىللەش ئۈچۈن بۇ تەكشۈرۈشنى تاماملاڭ.` until that count succeeds.

The cookie is `kb_visit`, `HttpOnly`, `Secure`, `SameSite=Lax`, `Path=/`, and `Domain=.kutadgubilik.com` on the production hosts. It carries a server UUID, not the client `visitor_id`. Verification stays valid for 12 hours. The cookie itself lasts 180 days so a later proof can keep the same identity. Deleting the cookie, using another browser, or an automated client that can pass Turnstile and store cookies remains a limitation. `cf_clearance`, `Origin`, `User-Agent`, and a client flag are not verification.

`STAGE125_VERIFIED_VISITS.sql` is manual and unapplied. It adds private verified receipts, a 3-hour gate, a proof-hash table, and an outage signal. Each signal covers `[occurred_at, observed_until)`. A later `kutadgu_note_verified_outage()` call while that coverage is still open extends `observed_until` instead of dropping the observation. Cards, charts, countries, and history treat an overlapping signal as partial, including a window that starts after `occurred_at` and a window that crosses midnight. Counting RPCs are `SECURITY INVOKER`, revoked from `PUBLIC`, `anon`, and `authenticated`, and granted to `service_role` only. Direct `analytics_events` inserts still create legacy receipts through the old trigger and do not create verified receipts. Cards, charts, country totals, and paginated history read `verified_visits` and `get_kutadgu_verified_visit_history`. `counted_visits` stays on the page as `كونا ئىستاتىستىكا` and is not relabeled. A missing marker is unavailable. A quiet covered interval is zero. A proof-consumption RPC that returns a service error, or throws, answers 503 `verification_unavailable` and records an outage. A counting RPC that returns a service error, or throws, does the same and does not count. An invalid token stays `verification_rejected` and a replayed proof stays `replayed_proof`; neither is recorded as an outage. Historical rows are not backfilled. Rollback is `STAGE125_VERIFIED_VISITS_ROLLBACK.sql`.

The production Worker now has plain-text `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` (`0x4AAAAAAFYwa2nBmJP1tS5K`, not a `1x`, `2x`, or `3x` dummy key) and encrypted secrets `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`, `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY`, and `VISIT_SIGNING_SECRET`. The public site key is also in `wrangler.jsonc` under `env.production.vars`, so a later Wrangler deploy keeps the dashboard value. Preview vars do not include it. Secret values are not in the repository. Existing plain-text variables and the `COVERS` R2 binding are unchanged. Before any claim of protection, apply Stage 125, deploy this draft's application code after review, and confirm one real browser receipt. Report-only CSP adds only `https://challenges.cloudflare.com` to `script-src`, `connect-src`, and `frame-src`.

Local evidence uses a mocked Siteverify response in `scripts/verified-visits-tests.js`. That is not a live Turnstile call and not a physical device. The same file now covers a 503 from `kutadgu_consume_visit_proof`, a thrown proof RPC error, a thrown count RPC with a valid cookie, and an invalid token that must not record an outage. `node scripts/verified-visits-tests.js` passed on `e5c5539a3c288dfb23eea04db2590783247776d2`. `bash scripts/stage125-isolated-postgres.sh` passed on throwaway PostgreSQL 16, including the continuing-outage reproduction, the midnight boundary, grants, direct inserts, the 3-hour gate, concurrency, history snapshots, and rollback. `tests/e2e/verified-visit-proof.spec.js` passed in Chromium: the Turnstile object there is mocked and marked `data-mocked-turnstile`, the panel is removed after a successful proof and count, `analyticsHooks: false` makes no visit request and does not load Turnstile, and localhost without the exercise attribute does not count. The header diagnostic still recorded no posts. Those browser checks are not a physical device. Stage 10 run [38947945233](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/38947945233) succeeded on `853c48b0e42a56778308a9cf2e94321ff63cc571`: 1062 passed and 1 flaky. The flaky case was `tests/e2e/book-detail-google-entry.spec.js` for the root listing; its retry passed. It does not exercise visit proof. Production protection remains unconfigured and unverified.

## iPhone Instagram header flow — released PR #293

The owner confirmed the header behaves normally after PR #293. That confirmation is not a proven rendering root cause. The rest of this section records the draft before that release.

Draft branch `cursor/instagram-header-flow-e5d6` and draft PR #293 keep the public header in normal flow on iPhone Instagram. The application commit is `9e484d681eff70c8e25fc4521f4d16e85acde5fb`. It is not deployed. `origin/main` is the PR #292 merge `167b733cfbec42c89d48660c7220bcd879add64b`. Production Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `07528a2c-923e-40ca-83b3-92b48095be93` is deployment `a7bcd655-957c-4453-b2d7-c0e96e839d15` at 100%, created 2026-10-16T09:27:50.444916Z. The rollback target before any deploy of this draft is that deployment and version. The sentences below that still call PR #292 an undeployed draft, and that still name production as the PR #291 Worker `fd069610-2f29-4e42-a440-e51e30cda37e`, describe that work before its release.

The owner captured the homepage inside Instagram while the brown header was visibly lower and hero content showed above it. The diagnostic on that same screen reported `headerTop` 0. Earlier manual snapshots also reported top 0. Layout geometry is not evidence that the bar is where the owner sees it, and this draft does not treat the incident as recovered.

The fallback runs only when the user agent contains both `iPhone` and `Instagram`. At 768px and below, `header.is-mobile-enhanced` and `.mobile-site-header` use `position: sticky` instead of `position: fixed`. `body.has-mobile-fixed-header` padding-top is 0 in both `mobile.css` and `public-header.css`, including the more specific `body.has-kutadgu-public-header.has-mobile-fixed-header` rule, so the in-flow bar does not leave a second header-height gap. The header still includes its own safe-area padding. Search, cart, and the menu stay in place. The bottom bar stays `position: fixed`. The menu-open override is `overflow: clip` in `public-header.css`, because that file is what switches the bar to sticky. A cached `mobile.css` can still say `overflow: hidden`, and that combination moved the sticky bar to header top -650 while `scrollY` stayed 650. Putting the override only in the newer `mobile.css` left that mix broken. A wheel still moves the page behind the open menu on both `main` and this draft, so clip is not a new background lock and this fix does not add one. iPhone Safari, Android Instagram, and desktop keep the previous positioning. Desktop blur stays. There is no `scrollTo` loop and no `visualViewport` translation. This is a compatibility mitigation. It is not a proven WebKit cause. If the physical bar is still painted away from its box, the simpler next fallback is normal-flow static positioning, which would let the header scroll away.

`index.html` requests `mobile.css?v=27` and `public-header.js?v=5`. That script requests `public-header.css?v=8`. Other storefront documents still request `public-header.js?v=4`. A cached copy of that URL does not add `kutadgu-ig-iphone` until it is fetched again. SHA-256 is `index.html` `dd89a1f5bd1673b6ab054ef2293523b4f1c718a65096e609cd93e568f866f847` (29794 bytes), `mobile.css` `a0685e8a94220422b380b926c52ccdb1eaad3e1209b0ba82e48a9c593803c155` (36240 bytes), `public-header.css` `e08b75538abda94b58f385b293a583105b6ba66e984fbfd1e1edfe53f43c9ebc` (15051 bytes), and `public-header.js` `b934cade9ae01efdbd1d93254fc4b490cca42327c9b35653d16391f45821579d` (17279 bytes). `shop.js?v=163` is unchanged. No SQL and no security rule was changed.

The owner refreshed the homepage on an iPhone inside Instagram after that deploy and the brown header still moved downward. No screenshot, WebView log, or copied page state was available here. Playwright on this machine did not reproduce the displacement, and that result does not resolve the phone. A read of the Worker origin on 2026-10-16 showed `/` as `public, max-age=0, must-revalidate`. `mobile.css?v=26` and `mobile.css?v=25` both returned the released file, 35576 bytes, SHA-256 `7f6f9b26b2da44dccb6aea87a064bbc5acf18f8a35023eb70f7302525b37378f`, with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=300, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400`. Asset paths keep an allowlisted file name and a numeric `v` value. An empty stylesheet address is not turned into the current page URL. The panel records load, `pageshow`, resize, scroll, and a manual tap. The buffer stays at 16 snapshots. A new load, `pageshow`, or `DOMContentLoaded` record replaces a repeated scroll, resize, or manual row instead of being discarded. It stores the header and mobile asset paths, matched header rules, the header rectangle, computed position, top, insets, backdrop filters, body padding, root and ancestor overflow and transforms, scroll position, and `visualViewport` offset, size, and scale. The owner copies the text from the page. The script does not read an account, cookie, token, or IP, and it does not send the report. It does not change header positioning or call `scrollTo`. Desktop blur, catalog recovery, menu, and bottom navigation stay as they are.

## Catalog read recovery — released PR #290

PR #290 merged as `3037ad7bc575b35b9e69cb22f47338e6a8038954` at 2026-10-15T20:56:23Z. Its tree matches reviewed head `70bdb412da625a281ed8ce165479fb4590a45bea`. The tested application commit is `bb09946402eb605cd71f6f24a3f9c4b710ab06f6`. Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `948fdee1-be72-42f4-b49e-52f18ed6ea05` is the current production deploy. The sentences below that still call this pull request an undeployed draft, and the sections that still call PR #289 a draft or name `origin/main` as `cd548bc0ad71c7f450f5c68f50d50fd4f8cc5197`, describe those drafts before release. Both releases are deployed. The mobile-header draft above does not change this catalog behavior.

The owner's iPhone showed loading errors for recommended books, recently added books, and category discovery on Wi-Fi, and loaded books on cellular. VPN was off, and no new protection setting was enabled for that check. Independent public reads returned 544 active books and an HTTP 206 catalog page. Those reads were not the phone's Wi-Fi path. The reproducible code fault is separate: the 8-second availability HEAD can set `remoteCatalog.available` false, and `queryCatalog` then throws without a catalog fetch until `loadRemoteCatalog` runs again. This draft does not raise that timeout. A configured query now makes its normal bounded page request after the probe fails. A successful page opens the catalog. A failed page stays an error. An empty page stays empty and is not filled with sample books. A two-second cooldown applies only after a failed recovery read, so the homepage sections do not each start another automatic burst. `قايتا سىناش` bypasses that cooldown and repeats the current carousel mode, recently added query, discovery group, or listing filters. A newer category or sort replaces an older in-flight response. Prices, stock, hidden rows, and the anonymous publishable key are unchanged. If the phone's Wi-Fi still cannot reach the catalog host, retry shows the same error. This draft does not fix that reachability failure.

Storefront documents request `shop.js?v=163`. `shop.js` requests `premium-ux.js?v=15`. SHA-256 is `shop.js` `def757b5c80475216c175537dd4a7305c9516f46d617f7d978f89d3d99274341` (274314 bytes), `premium-ux.js` `c1aea04769a9bf7d471d1eb3ea1abd7e37c915b85774f736288999dea02a6304` (29111 bytes), `index.html` `b23debe50725499969287554f9aa05f094cfb5b7a69a19b928b9b952b0d1f741` (29745 bytes), and `book-shell.html` `3c704e43e5eeb20795c535954a2ae3f6b6ed7d810e36df8b366547c53af86f18` (12886 bytes).

Local evidence on application commit `bb09946402eb605cd71f6f24a3f9c4b710ab06f6`, with the spec assertions in the commit that records this paragraph. `node scripts/listing-catalog-boot-tests.js`, `scripts/homepage-catalog-boot-tests.js`, and `scripts/homepage-discovery-authoritative-tests.js` passed. `scripts/homepage-compact-ux-tests.js` still fails its existing `shop.js?v=148` assertion and the old `resolveInitialMode().then` assertion. Those strings are already absent on `origin/main`. `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1 KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173 npx playwright test tests/e2e/catalog-probe-recovery.spec.js --reporter=line` passed 7 tests. The cases cover a failed probe followed by healthy bounded reads, the real 8-second probe abort, a slow read, a healthy cold load with one availability probe, a permanent failure, explicit Uyghur retry, an empty remote page, a discovery-group change while a read is held, and a listing sort change while a read is held. Chromium viewports were 390×844 and 1280×844. That is emulation, not a physical iPhone, and not the owner's Wi-Fi. No production SQL, security rule, deployment, or analytics write was made.

## Last visit country — draft, not deployed

The tested application commit is `62ac00eaffcfab0d7cc1bfb6949eabea2e304ac3`. `origin/main` remains `cd548bc0ad71c7f450f5c68f50d50fd4f8cc5197`, the merge of PR #288. Password and Google signup stay on the native SDK. `member.js` no longer rewrites `POST /auth/v1/signup`. An authenticated visit, including a restored session, calls the existing 60-second `recordVisit` path once. That path posts `page_path` to same-origin `POST /api/member-visit` with the session bearer. It does not send a user id, country, or timestamp, and it does not also call `record_member_visit`. The post is sent only when `getSession` returns a session whose `user.id` is the captured member and the application user is still that member. A pending account-A visit therefore does not send account B's token or write A's cadence key. The cadence key is written only after HTTP success, so a failed write can retry without a second counted visit. Logout during an already-sent success still stores that member's cadence key. A suspended profile is signed out before the post. The suspended notice appears, and that local sign-out then returns the account to the signed-out guest state. The account is not left blocked.

The Worker reads `request.cf.country` through the existing trusted-country helper. It takes the observation time at request entry, before reading the body or verifying the bearer, and sends that same timestamp to SQL. It verifies the bearer with GoTrue and ignores a body user id, body country, body time, `X-Forwarded-For`, and `CF-IPCountry`. The service-role call is one `record_member_last_visit` with the verified user id, the bounded page, the trusted country or null, and that entry timestamp. A missing, `XX`, or otherwise unusable country stores null for that visit. An older observation does not replace a newer country, observation time, `last_seen_at`, or `last_page`, and it still increments `visit_count` once. An earlier request that finishes after a later request keeps its earlier timestamp, so it cannot replace the later country, time, or page. The same transaction updates the profile activity row only when the observation is newer than both `member_last_visit.observed_at` and `profiles.last_seen_at`. `profiles.country` is not written. A rejected or expired token returns 401 and does not write. A failed write returns 503 and is not repeated inside the Worker.

Cached clients can still call `record_member_visit(text)`. That function stays executable by `authenticated` and not by `anon`. It uses the same per-user advisory lock. A newer legacy visit has no country evidence: it stores a null country, sets `observed_at` to its own visit time, and advances `last_seen_at` and `last_page`. An older legacy call only increments `visit_count`. The directory returns the stored country only when that observation is at least as new as `profiles.last_seen_at`. A newer visit without country evidence therefore shows `نامەلۇم` instead of an older country. Each accepted call increments `visit_count` once. Rollback restores the original `record_member_visit(text)` body before dropping the new table.

The value lives in `public.member_last_visit`, not in `profiles.country`. The table has forced row-level security and no privileges for `anon`, `authenticated`, or `service_role`. Only `service_role` can execute `record_member_last_visit`. Admin reads stay inside `admin_member_directory_page`, which still requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal2`. The directory signature is unchanged. The visit country is not a search field. The visible label is `ئاخىرقى زىيارەت دۆلىتى`, followed by the observation time when one exists. Names come from `KutadguAnalyticsCore.countryLabel`. Null, `XX`, a raw two-letter code such as `QQ`, and any other unsupported label show `نامەلۇم`. A historical member with no accepted visit stays `نامەلۇم`. This is the network country of the latest accepted visit, not residence, nationality, or registration country. No raw IP address and no country history are stored.

`account.html`, `feedback.html`, `book-staff.html`, and the `shop.js` loader request `member.js?v=34`. Storefront documents that load `shop.js` request `shop.js?v=162`. `admin.html` requests `kutadgu-member-page-label.js?v=1` and `admin.js?v=113`. `member.js` SHA-256 is `43d10db2bae1ee8629be8602c4f292b2aff40a8d7e65d1f7315c893aee6402c4` (48858 bytes). `shop.js` SHA-256 is `722449c2e636900a242e08b3b0b995e4970926b32b75a9aa65ceaf0560f4e457` (272879 bytes). `index.html` SHA-256 is `ad5034a7e48d550196f08258562b3eee33a94bba5190e20e4786eb2ea5940bbd` (29745 bytes). `book-shell.html` SHA-256 is `f9e69184ce67bfe2e9f9221b6ebf0d1f52a4a0a897bf5bf4b299bd235fd0f8e9` (12886 bytes). The warmed `member.js?v=31` fixture remains the `origin/main` file, SHA-256 `0ada94414aa3e76ca3aedb1c12db3c29b8d3a0982ad1fc0df3046b58c6451bc8`. Historical warm-cache fixtures stay.

The stored `last_page` value is unchanged. The directory shows a closed Uyghur name: `cart.html` is `سېۋەت`, `account.html` is `ئەزالىق بېتى`, and `index.html` or `/` is `باش بەت`. Book, favorites, category, author, translator, publisher, and the existing public info routes use the same helper. Query strings, fragments, external hosts, and hostile strings become `نامەلۇم`. Names, emails, phones, and the member-edited country stay as entered. The member-cart heading is the static `سېۋەت`.

An older new visit after a legacy visit did not move them backward and still counted once. A later country visit then replaced the null country. Overlapping legacy and new calls kept the newer country when the new timestamp was later, kept Unknown when the legacy visit was later, and incremented `visit_count` by one for each call. Authenticated can still execute `record_member_visit(text)`; anon cannot. Rollback restored the original recorder, and that restored function counted one profile visit. Reapply recorded again. Profile country stayed editable. Historical rows stayed null. The code is not searchable. Paging stayed 20 rows. Anon and authenticated cannot read or write the new table. ## Homepage web app manifest — merged PR #288

PR #288 merged as `cd548bc0ad71c7f450f5c68f50d50fd4f8cc5197`. The earlier sentence that called this an undeployed draft is stale. A read-only GET on 2026-10-14 showed `/kutadgu.webmanifest` linked from the live homepage. This session did not deploy a Worker and did not freshly verify a Worker version. Historical release evidence for #288, not rechecked here, names Worker `32fde109-a76d-444e-b65d-a226a024b08e` and deployment `be999de0-1af5-44c5-826a-c56d72848f7c`. The gift-label Worker `25ff5020-3e2e-4d3d-8feb-5f0aa202b150` on deployment `26a8bae5-c07c-4169-9798-93f9794e6dc0` remains the earlier PR #287 release record.

The merged change adds one web app manifest and links it from `index.html` only. The manifest id, start URL, and scope are `/`. Display is `standalone`. Language is `ug` and direction is `rtl`. The name and short name are `قۇتادغۇبىلىك كىتابخانىسى`. Background `#f6f0e5` and theme `#4b3327` match the light storefront tokens. The only icon is the existing `/kutadgu-logo.png`, type `image/png`, with `sizes` set to `any`. That keyword is the metadata correction. The file remains a 1254×1254 PNG, not a vector, and no 192 or 512 size is declared. Orientation is not locked. There is no service worker, offline cache, install banner, or `apple-touch-icon`. The favicon check still requires exactly one `rel=icon` and rejects an Apple touch icon, so that assertion was left unchanged. `.well-known/assetlinks.json` and `.well-known/apple-app-site-association.json` are unchanged. `shop.js?v=160` and `admin.js?v=111` are unchanged.

`kutadgu.webmanifest` SHA-256 is `8508a003f8d413e8bd038f9edbbf84bc67ebc47daacbe0a6b652fe2b16126307` (412 bytes). The draft `index.html` SHA-256 is `ac9f21ed661b6acd6f70efb70fc3ebcab2b3b0530db36ac97db88a5974b81be2`. The logo SHA-256 stays `ca0afbb2b5f4a7552073520c13215cfbf4254eb5a81eca7ac0b53b10f6e777c9` (941590 bytes, PNG IHDR 1254×1254). The local fixture server maps `.webmanifest` to `application/manifest+json`. Wrangler already uses that type for the extension, so the Worker was not changed and security headers were not relaxed.

The first draft reported an empty `Page.getInstallabilityErrors` list from Playwright's Chromium `151.0.7922.34`. That browser did not report the icon failure. Google Chrome `154.0.8037.92`, in a fresh ordinary persistent profile and not an incognito window, returned `manifest-missing-suitable-icon` with `minimum-icon-size-in-pixels` `144` while `sizes` was `1254x1254`. The same PNG still decoded at 1254×1254. Declaring `sizes` as `any` clears that error. Chromium's parsed icon record then shows `sizes` `0x0`, which is how this browser encodes `any`. It is not the bitmap size. The downloaded response stays `image/png`, and both the IHDR and the decoded image stay 1254×1254.

On that Chrome profile, the real `beforeinstallprompt` event opened the browser's Install app dialog. An ordinary click on its Install button returned `userChoice` `accepted` and fired `appinstalled`. Chrome stored the app as `mobfckblkbpnblbfdlcpeglhccehnjmo` and kept a generated icon whose IHDR is 1254×1254. After the browser was closed, `google-chrome --app-id=mobfckblkbpnblbfdlcpeglhccehnjmo` opened one window at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/` with `display-mode: standalone`. That launch is the installed app id, not an `--app` URL. Inside that window, with the local read-safe fixtures, the homepage opened book `626`, the cart showed that book and a lira price, favorites opened, and `account.html#signup` showed `#signupForm` with `#loginForm` hidden. The signup form was not submitted and WhatsApp was not opened. Service-worker registrations and Cache Storage keys stayed empty. This is Linux Chrome on this machine. It is not a physical phone, not an iPhone, and not a Windows or Android device.

Local Playwright at `http://127.0.0.1:4173` with `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1` passed `tests/e2e/web-app-manifest.spec.js`: 2 tests. The ordinary tab parsed `sizes` `any`, saw Chromium's parsed `0x0`, decoded the logo at 1254×1254, and found no service worker. At 1280 and 390, in light and dark, the homepage opened a featured book, the detail cart control added that book, the cart showed the existing WhatsApp control, favorites opened, and `account.html#signup` showed `#signupForm` with `#loginForm` hidden. A later offline navigation to the cart failed before a document loaded. `node scripts/web-app-manifest-tests.js` and `node scripts/cloudflare-preview-tests.js` passed. `node scripts/stage-seo-2h-category-listing-seo-tests.js` still fails only the pre-existing `iqtisad` and `taamlar` checks; its frozen-file check passed on this revision. `scripts/stage-seo-2d-favicon-tests.js` still passes the single homepage favicon check and still fails its historical `shop.js` hash. Those sentences describe the manifest while PR #288 was still a draft. It later merged as `cd548bc0ad71c7f450f5c68f50d50fd4f8cc5197`. The runtime baseline is the table below.

The paragraphs below were written while PR #283 was still a draft.


This draft adds one `🎁 سوۋغا كىتابلار` tab to the existing homepage carousel. Signed-out visitors keep the current public initial tab. Opening the gift tab shows an account invitation to `account.html#signup` and does not read the offer list. A confirmed active member sees available gifts first. A successful empty gift read falls through to recommended books, then to another nonempty public tab. A failed gift read stays on the gift tab with `قايتا سىناش` and is not treated as an empty list. A manual tab choice wins over a late response. Logout, a suspended or missing profile, and an account change clear protected gift cards. Gift rows are not stored in `localStorage`, `sessionStorage`, or the shared anonymous catalog cache.

The books are free physical copies. Membership is enough. The request is a separate WhatsApp inquiry through the existing `wa.me` opener. It does not add a paid cart line, create a paid order, or change `books.price`. Availability uses the book's existing stock and manual stock status. A request does not reserve or decrement stock.

`STAGE122_GIFT_BOOK_OFFERS.sql` adds `public.gift_book_offers` with `book_id bigint`, `enabled`, and `updated_at`. Row level security is enabled and forced. `anon` and `service_role` have no privileges. An active member can read only enabled offers whose book is active, approved, and still available. An administrator with `is_kutadgu_admin()` and AAL2 can read disabled offers and is the only writer. The live `books` select policy is not replaced. The matching rollback drops only the new table and functions. This draft does not apply that SQL and does not change production permissions.

The storefront pin is `shop.js?v=158`. `admin.html` requests `admin.js?v=110`. SHA-256 of this pagination correction is `shop.js` `3120a692648db2ed6f7072d067c643f31069d419ffe0c464c243335c03c2fd69` (272760 bytes), `index.html` `3ddb6d9478b8c5ed932dd2a0b849da15b027d2219c4986b4868efb08a1f9c319` (29687 bytes), and `book-shell.html` `56bd5c961657ba620d157de0abfc37f5e083aa54c2ec1a8a7e7b727b70a631ae` (12886 bytes). The previous correction pin was `shop.js?v=157`, SHA-256 `6f30434fc12194208404bf0cabad266a7af835d49eef645d8daa8345ccad028d` (272108 bytes), with `index.html` `afedafd98626175b9e81a81c0fdbfaad93388e585a445df6a08843f4b672dfd0` and `book-shell.html` `b65cf861a99f5dab6d45ba2f38f60770684505b813ac61bb90e0974b429a3bd3`. The earlier draft pins were `shop.js?v=156` and `admin.js?v=109`. The runtime baseline stays the released PR #281 merge `94094934ea801741043fdf1362afc31f3ec7b6f7`. Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `50770f4b-cc21-45e7-993d-3ebc3560efb3` is unchanged. No production SQL was applied.

Gift cards stay gift cards on every carousel redraw. `card()` follows `mode==="gifts"`, and a resize does not replace an invitation, empty, loading, unavailable, or error surface. The gift heart hydrates the public book through `hydrateBooksByIds` and does not put the offer row or the `price:null` projection into the shared catalog cache. A heart click that arrives before that public book is indexed is applied only if the same account is still current. A failed or unread admin gift load does not write an offer and does not keep an ordinary book save open. A new unchecked book skips the offer write even when the saved id is still empty. A real offer write failure still keeps the modal open. `scripts/stage122-isolated-verify.js` now also starts throwaway PostgREST and checks the member embed and the admin insert/update requests over HTTP.

A public carousel append that is still in flight cannot change the tab the visitor has since selected. `next()` captures the mode and `modeRequestId` before `loadMode`. It updates the list, redraws, and moves only when that same mode is still selected and the request generation is still the one `loadMode` started. `setMode` increments that generation and aborts the previous catalog request, including a switch to gifts and a switch to a cached public tab. Logout and an account change do the same before clearing gift cards. A superseded page is not written into the mode cache. Same-tab recommended pagination still appends. Only the current eligible offer rows are rendered as gifts.

Draft PR #283 on `cursor/gift-book-offers-e3d6` carries this feature. The implementation commit is `55141e51b29fd3dcf45bc54dc4d56d7c25e23849`. The first verification commit is `3b58b24d853a4ab080630702623c902a34f09195`. The redraw, favorite, and admin-save correction is `eec7919466d577b3953f5031a7754d649b544707`. The stale-pagination correction is `12090d90865459dae01f6a995c6eb0a1a0a1d0f9`. The rollback drops `gift_book_offers` before `gift_book_is_requestable(bigint)`, because the member policy depends on that function. It still drops only the new table and the two new functions.

The first isolated run used throwaway PostgreSQL on port 55482 and did not open project `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh`. It exited 0 with 20 SQL passes and did not start PostgREST. The correction run uses the same throwaway database and PostgREST 16.4 from `/tmp/postgrest`, outside the checkout. `STAGE122_PG_MODULES=/tmp/stage122-pg/node_modules POSTGREST_BIN=/tmp/postgrest node scripts/stage122-isolated-verify.js` exited 0 with 33 passes. Repeat apply kept the books policy `((is_active = true) AND (submission_status = 'approved'::text))`. Anon is denied in SQL and over HTTP, and the HTTP body is only `permission denied for table gift_book_offers`. An active member HTTP read returns enabled requestable offers joined to `books` (`available`, `unconfigured`, and `new gift`). A suspended profile and a missing profile return `[]`. Anonymous `books?select=*` returns the public columns `author,id,image_url,is_active,price,stock,stock_status,submission_status,title`, including a title whose offer is disabled, and it does not return pending or inactive titles and has no gift column. Member and admin AAL1 HTTP inserts return 403 and add no row. Admin AAL2 HTTP insert returns 201 with `enabled:true`. Admin AAL2 HTTP update with the current `updated_at` returns the disabled row. A stale `updated_at` returns `[]` and leaves the row disabled. Rollback keeps books and the approved books policy. Reapply leaves an empty offer table.

The notice is the existing `.checkout-member-note`, now `#guestCartInvite`, moved from inside `#checkoutCard` to `#cartAside` so a stock-blocked checkout does not hide it. It starts hidden. The action is `ئەزا بولۇش` and its href is `account.html#signup`. `guestCartInviteDecision` shows it only after a member event, a finished configured session boot, no auth error, no user, profile, blocked flag, held account, persisted user, or pending bootstrap, owner `guest`, and a displayed cart length above zero. Empty carts, authenticated members, loading, missing, and suspended profiles, unresolved boot, and failed authentication hide it. A foreign owner is not treated as a guest, and that retained cart is not displayed. Checkout stock guards are unchanged. Guest browsing, the cart, and WhatsApp ordering stay available. There is no second invitation, popup, modal, or new auth client.

`account.js` treats only the exact hash `#signup` as a signup entry. The first unauthenticated paint uses it. A later `showUnauthenticatedAccount()` keeps signup only while the visitor has not chosen a tab. Choosing login, a successful member render, or the existing email-confirmation branch sets that choice and is not forced back to signup. Ordinary `account.html` still opens login. No return URL and no authentication callback were added.

No new storage key, save path, table, or migration was added. `member.js` is unchanged and stays `member.js?v=31`. Signup that returns a session still queues the existing merge. Signup without a session still shows the existing confirmation sentence, which does not say the cart is saved, and the local cart remains. A later login in the same browser still follows the existing merge. Quantities still collapse with the existing maximum, capped at 99. A steady foreign cart stays excluded. An existing `member.js` behavior remains: during a foreign-owner merge, a local quantity change or a new book made in that window is still upserted onto the other account’s result. This draft records that with a unit test and does not repair it. Guest books are not promised to move to another device before a successful authenticated synchronization.

Each book is one logical line: `1. ئاچىل — 2 دانە · 306 ₺`. The amount is quantity times the unit price, shown once. The greeting and `زاكاز نومۇرى: KB-...` stay. After the list, one WhatsApp-bold line reads `*جەمئىي كىتاب سانى: 7 — جەمئىي: 1.577,20 ₺*`. The quantity in that line is the sum of quantities. Integer line subtotals stay compact, such as `153 ₺` and `294 ₺`. A fractional line subtotal uses two Turkish decimal places, such as `824,20 ₺`. The grand total always uses two decimal places, such as `11.234,30 ₺`. Those helpers are `orderMessageAmount`, `orderItemAmount`, and `orderGrandAmount` inside `buildOrderText`. `money()` is unchanged, so cards, the cart page, account, and admin prices stay on the existing formatter. Full titles stay on the logical line. `shop.css` still wraps the preview with `white-space: pre-wrap`. A note is included only when it has non-whitespace text. Name, phone, city/region, address, and delivery stay, including their existing dash when empty. The examples use synthetic customer data: name `Aygul Test`, phone `0555 000 11 11`, city `Istanbul / Fatih`, address `Test street 1, door 4`.

The structured payload still stores the unit `price`, `qty`, `line_total`, `total`, `totalQty`, customer, and book identity. Empty-cart, hydration, visibility, stock, quantity-clamp, and invalid-form guards still return no message. `getOrBuildOrder` still reuses `preparedOrder` and its `orderId` while the cart and saved customer are unchanged. Preview, Copy Order, Share Order, and WhatsApp read that same text. Saved order history is not rewritten. Cart-link permissions and public recipient imports from PR #277 stay as released.

Review implementation `273a78510f1119901a5020f4cca584cabdfb0f2d` closes sharing at the SDK identity change, caps the stored link lifetime, and reserves admin operation ownership. Review tests `b4f575a9dbceecc2abd0fe9b538411cc56a4cf8d` cover those cases. `c160c1879e69811cbd40d477c06a6e2cf40230ed` is documentation plus one test-guard change: it records this evidence and the PR #275 baseline, and it adds `docs/cart-share-stage121-verify.txt` to the allowed file list in `scripts/stage-seo-2h-category-listing-seo-tests.js`. `87614d9b60b884f58f0e1a59c922370a8cf80bb9` is documentation only. Stale-result tests `d857bcbcb09644a2c6257190e5eef23ac3a8e9d9` and `399790a8d3cf3423f5a65561d148942c0f4ac1ce` do not change application code. The application SHA remains `273a78510f1119901a5020f4cca584cabdfb0f2d`. The shared-cart paragraph in Important storefront behavior is the released creation path. This draft restricts creation only.

Default state is global sharing off and no individual grants. An active profile plus either global mode or a saved grant is required. Guests, a missing profile, and a suspended profile cannot create a link, including when global mode is on or a grant row remains. Admins are not granted automatically. Global mode does not write or clear saved grants. Turning it off returns to those grants. `member.js` keeps its existing auth, cart, favorites, and feedback sign-out behavior. The review adds one hook, `noteCartShareSignOut()`, immediately after `beginSignOut()` and before `abandonMemberShopSync()`. It does not snapshot or restore an old account or cart, and it does not create another auth client. `account.html`, `book-staff.html`, `feedback.html`, and the dynamic loader request `member.js?v=31`.

Permission storage is `public.cart_share_settings` and `public.cart_share_grants`, not a profile column. Both tables have row level security enabled and forced, and no privileges for `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`, or `service_role`. `my_cart_share_permission()` returns a boolean for `auth.uid()` only. A null user, a missing or non-active profile, a missing settings row, or a missing grant returns false. `admin_cart_share_state(uuid[])`, `admin_set_cart_share_global(boolean)`, and `admin_set_cart_share_grant(uuid, boolean)` require `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal` exactly `aal2`. The state function accepts at most 20 ids and also returns the caller's own id, saved grant, and eligibility. The directory query still excludes `auth.uid()`. The signed-in admin grants their own account from a separate control in the same Members section.

`create_member_shared_cart_link(text, jsonb, timestamptz)` is the authenticated creation function. It is security definer, `search_path = pg_catalog, public`, and executable by `authenticated` only. It reads `auth.uid()` from the PostgREST-verified JWT. It takes advisory lock `841211211`, then locks the caller profile, the settings row, and any saved grant before deciding. A revoke or a global-mode change uses that same lock first, so it cannot commit between the check and the insert. After permission passes, the existing rate lock `841050105` and the Stage 105 validation, expiry delete, collision, and insert behavior still apply. Caller `p_expires_at` is checked and then discarded. Null, past, and non-future values return `invalid`. A value more than 30 days and 5 minutes ahead, including infinity, also returns `invalid` and stores nothing. An accepted value is replaced by the database clock plus 30 days, so a direct caller cannot store a longer lifetime. A denial inserts nothing and does not consume the rate cap. `create_shared_cart_link` stays `service_role` only. Link rows still store only the code, `{id, qty}` items, `created_at`, and `expires_at`.

The Worker `POST /api/shared-cart` forwards the caller bearer token with the public publishable key. A missing or malformed token returns 401 and does not call Supabase. A database `denied` result, or HTTP 401 or 403 from PostgREST, returns 403. A network failure, a malformed result, or another non-success returns 503. `GET /api/shared-cart/<code>` still uses the server secret. Recipients of a valid short link or a legacy `cart.html?share=` link can import without an account. Body size, item count, quantity, code generation, collision retry, the 30-day expiry, replay protection, stock limits, cart merging, and App Links stay as they are. WhatsApp and the order-text controls are separate and stay available.

The cart button stays out of the page until the current SDK identity, the established active member profile, and a confirmed permission agree. An SDK identity change closes sharing before the member session queue updates the facade. Sign-out closes sharing before `auth.signOut` finishes, including when that call is delayed or throws. The client rejects a session error and an owner mismatch before `POST`. A temporary permission-read failure stays unready and retries at most three times for that same identity. A late result for account A cannot show the button, create a link, copy it, show success, or clear a newer operation. Admin reads and writes reserve a unique operation before the first await. An older response cannot overwrite confirmed state or release a newer operation. An ambiguous write reloads `admin_cart_share_state`. Controls keep errors on `#cartShareStatus`. While global mode is on, individual controls show the saved grant as text and do not offer an off switch that would look like a block.

Storefront documents that load `shop.js`, including `cart.html`, `index.html`, and `book-shell.html`, request `shop.js?v=153`. `admin.html` requests `admin.js?v=108` and `admin.css?v=56`. Frozen SHA-256 values are `shop.js` `9d832a1c249bf92cc48358a3292372d6890b36b732ead01c7a90c6fa4bf9adfb`, `index.html` `8bed109b9a4bf650248acf6df74f8e73976d175f4215ec5d188512bbb5c2b211`, and `book-shell.html` `17de5b01de4b9ee091b567dd3c67a341c663b0b7cc2f4c46f4347713a30fd895`. `.js` cache policy is unchanged. No image, cover, logo, or banner file was modified.

`STAGE121_CART_SHARE_PERMISSIONS.sql` and `STAGE121_CART_SHARE_PERMISSIONS_ROLLBACK.sql` are new. Stages 105, 113, and 120 were not modified. Reapplying Stage 121 keeps the settings row and the grant rows. The rollback drops the five functions and both permission tables. That deletes saved grants and the global flag. It does not drop `shared_cart_links` or `create_shared_cart_link`. Reapplying after that rollback starts from global off and no grants. Link rows are not deleted by either file. The released Worker still creates links with the service key and `create_shared_cart_link`, and that route does not check the new permission. Applying Stage 121 alone adds `create_member_shared_cart_link` and does not close the old route. Deploying this Worker before Stage 121 makes creation fail closed because the new function is missing, while recipient `GET` of an existing link still uses the service key. Reverting to the old Worker restores anonymous service-backed creation even if Stage 121 is applied. Both the corrected SQL and this Worker have to be live before an eligible member can create a link through the new path.

This task applied no SQL to `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh` and deployed nothing. The owner account was not guessed from repository ownership, commit authors, or Cloudflare metadata, and no grant was seeded. Activation stays pending. After a reviewed release, an AAL2 admin opens Admin Members and uses the self control, or the row control when that verified account is listed, to grant the owner's own member account. Leave global mode off unless every current and future active member should be able to create links.

## Live catalog-credit SQL status — 2026-10-03

The user explicitly authorized executing the first Stage 106 SQL file on the live Supabase project `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh`. `STAGE106_CATALOG_CREDITS.sql` is now applied as migration `stage106_catalog_credits`. The book table and its existing author/translator/publisher values were verified before and after: 350 books, all active, and the same credit-text fingerprint. The new tables contain 270 identities and 837 links (350 author, 149 translator, 338 publisher). `catalog_credit_review` contains 27 rows: 9 author, 16 translator, and 2 publisher.

A real anonymous-role SELECT initially failed with `42501: permission denied for function is_kutadgu_admin`. The existing admin helper intentionally cannot be executed by anon. Migration `stage106_catalog_identity_public_read_policy` fixed only the new catalog identity policies: public linked identities use the active-book predicate, and admin/AAL2 access is a separate policy restricted to authenticated. No admin-helper EXECUTE grant was added. The same fix is committed in this draft branch's `STAGE106_CATALOG_CREDITS.sql` at `a8bb579993fe6e26e6edfbf2992ad519357dec4e`, so repeating that file does not restore the broken policy.

Verified against the live database with `SET LOCAL ROLE anon` in read-only transactions: 270 public identities and 837 joined public credits are readable; anon cannot INSERT identities, UPDATE credits, execute the admin helper, or execute the credit writer. All three new tables have enabled and forced RLS. The existing website code was not deployed and PR #241 was not merged. The website/application baseline remains `8cf75ee0a550b34285c2f3875e91fef2d723e325`; the prior live website code remains as recorded below.

A later read of the same live project, without re-running the correction file, found `STAGE106_CATALOG_CREDIT_CORRECTIONS.sql` already applied. `catalog_credit_correction_map` has 21 rows, row level security enabled, and row level security forced. There is no policy on that table. `anon` receives 401 for the map, for `catalog_credit_review`, for `INSERT` on `catalog_identities`, and for `apply_catalog_credit_corrections()`. The live function body contains the book-row lock, `catalog_identity_key`, and `skipped-stale`. `anon`, `authenticated`, and PUBLIC cannot execute it. Current counts are 304 identities and 865 credits (360 author, 167 translator, 338 publisher). Three review rows remain, and none of them match the map: publisher book 353 `شىنجاڭ خەلق باش نەشرىياتى، قەشقەر ئۇيغۇر نەشرىياتى`, publisher book 352 `شىنجاڭ خەلق نەشرىياتى، قەشقەر ئۇيغۇر نەشرىياتى`, and translator book 122 `نۇرمۇھەممەت دۆلەتى، ھەلىمە مۇھەممەت`. That translator spelling is not the mapped `نۇرمۇھەممەد دۆلەتى، ھەلىمە مۇھەممەت`. Book 252 still stores `ئەنۋەر جاپپار، پەرھات جىلانوۋ، قادىر قاۋۇز` and the three author links in that order. Book 261 stores `شەھىدە، خەدىچە` and the two translator links in that order. The two `ENABLE` and `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` statements for the correction map are now in the repository file. They were already true on the live table, so they were not executed again. The earlier note that the correction function was absent is no longer the live state. ## Project overview

Uyghur-language bookstore storefront. Static HTML, CSS, and JavaScript on Vercel, with the live catalog, accounts, orders, and covers in Supabase. The public UI is RTL. Customers browse, search, and send orders through WhatsApp. There is no in-site card checkout.

Repository: `kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi`. Default branch: `main`.

## Production website

- Canonical site: `https://www.kutadgubilik.com` (`KUTADGU_SITE_ORIGIN` in `supabase-config.js`)
- Live `www` and the apex are Cloudflare Worker Custom Domains on `kutadgu-cloudflare-production`. The apex still returns 308 to `https://www.kutadgubilik.com`.
- Apex `kutadgubilik.com` auth callbacks are sent to the `www` host
- `kutadgu-bilig-kitab.vercel.app` is not a production auth, analytics, or PostHog host. Live traffic stays on the Cloudflare custom domains.

## Main stack

- Storefront: static pages (`index.html`, category hubs, `book-shell.html`) plus `shop.js`
- Book URLs: `/book/<id>` is served by `api/book-public.js` through `vercel.json`
- Catalog and accounts: Supabase Auth, Postgres, and Storage (`book-covers`)
- Optional product analytics: PostHog, proxied under `/kbg/` in `vercel.json`
- Tests: Node unit scripts (`npm run test:unit`) and Playwright (`tests/e2e`)
- Node used by Stage 10 CI: 22

## GitHub, Vercel, and Supabase

- GitHub `main` is what Vercel production deploys. Promoting an older Vercel deployment rolls back code only. It does not roll back Supabase.
- SQL files in the repo are manual history. Do not execute them against production from an agent task unless the task explicitly says to.
- Public Supabase project ref, already recorded in `STAGE11_RECOVERY.md`: `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh`
- The browser uses the public URL and publishable key in `supabase-config.js`. That file must not gain a service-role key.
- Important tables called out in `STAGE11_RECOVERY.md`: `books`, `admin_users`, `profiles`, `member_favorites`, `member_cart_items`, `orders`, `analytics_events`, plus Supabase `auth.users`.
- Cover and gallery bytes live in the public `book-covers` bucket. Postgres backups do not include those file bytes.

## Important storefront behavior

- Search, category, and listing queries go through Supabase REST from `shop.js`. If Supabase is unavailable, `catalog.js` is only a small static fallback, not the live catalog. A configured remote failure stays an error. It does not switch to those demo books.
- The unfiltered `/books` listing defaults to `بۇ قېتىملىق بايقاش تەرتىپى`. That order is one permutation of the eligible catalog for the current tab visit, stored in `sessionStorage` (`kutadgu-books-visit-v1`). Category pages, the homepage, related books, and favorites do not use it. Search and the named sorts keep their own order. Returning to the default sort in the same visit restores the same order.
- ISBN search uses the ISBN column only when the whole query is shaped like an ISBN: digits, `X`, spaces, or hyphens, and the digit length is exactly 10 or 13.
- Book JSON-LD `isbn` is emitted only for a checksum-valid ISBN-10, or an ISBN-13 with prefix 978 or 979 that is not in the 9790 music range. `gtin13` is that same ISBN-13. A failed check omits both fields and does not substitute a SKU. The visible ISBN row still shows the stored number after spaces and hyphens are removed. These checks do not prove the number was assigned to that book.
- Public pages request active books. Anonymous clients are not given inactive rows. RLS `public can read active books` is `is_active = true`.
- Bestsellers depend on `sales_count > 0`. The homepage shares one in-flight count and keeps it for about 10 minutes. A WhatsApp click does not increment `sales_count`.
- `is_recommended` and `is_new` are admin choices, not the bestseller source.
- View counts render from `book_view_stats.total_views` only when that total is more than 20 (`THRESHOLD` in `kutadgu-book-views.js`). A total of 20 stays hidden. Completed reads stay cached for 10 minutes. Concurrent reads of the same book id or the same normalized id set share one request. A superseded read does not paint and does not hide the count. The newest read paints its own total, including a lower correction, even when a later join of the older request holds the refresh token. A failed or missing current read is not cached and still hides the count. Local preview hosts (`localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`) skip view-stat reads. `book-shell.html` requests `kutadgu-book-views.js?v=6` directly and still requests `shop.js?v=145`. Homepage, listing, and other storefront documents that load the helper through `shop.js` request `shop.js?v=146`, and that file requests `kutadgu-book-views.js?v=6`. `cart.html` stays on `shop.js?v=141`. PR #265 is merged and deployed. The tested application commit is `782ed6889164598533e938e890068695cfa2a558`. The reviewed head is `e536b11f7f956e1c792a36d7eed70b0d44785b62`. Stage 10 run `37697814221`, job `113053764919`, passed on that exact head: 865 passed, 3 skipped. The deployed pins and Worker version are in the baseline section below.
- Stock enforcement defaults on (`KUTADGU_STOCK_ENFORCEMENT`). Prepared orders do not reserve stock. Admin commit deducts it.
- Guest cart and favorites stay in browser localStorage (`kutadgu-cart-v1`, `kutadgu-favorites-v1`). Logged-in member cart and favorites use Supabase.
- Shared cart v1 remains a website link with no database row: `https://www.kutadgubilik.com/cart.html?share=v1.<id>x<qty>.<id>x<qty>`. That payload is only canonical numeric book ids and quantities from 1 to 99, at most 80 unique ids. Opening it still merges into the recipient cart after the live catalog is ready, using the current price and stock, then `history.replaceState` removes `share`.
- New shares from `📤 سېۋەتنى ھەمبەھىرلەش` create an 8-character code and return `https://www.kutadgubilik.com/c/<code>`. `POST /api/shared-cart` and `GET /api/shared-cart/<code>` run in the Worker with `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`. The browser never receives that key. `/c/<code>` serves `cart.html` without redirecting to the long query. The same merge path preserves the existing cart, sums duplicate quantities, clamps to live stock, and skips unknown or unavailable ids. A refresh in the same tab does not import that code again; `sessionStorage` key `kutadgu-shared-cart-short-v1` records the code, and the address bar stays `/c/<code>`. A later visit in a new tab can import it again until the row expires. A link stays valid for 30 days. WhatsApp and other link previews use the `cart.html` meta description `ھەمبەھىرلەنگەن كىتابلارنى كۆرۈش ئۈچۈن ئۇلانمىنى ئېچىڭ.` The robots meta stays `noindex, follow`. `create_shared_cart_link` deletes expired rows in the same transaction as a successful insert. The function also allows at most 300 new links every 10 minutes and 1000 new links every 24 hours for the whole site, using a transaction advisory lock and the table's own `created_at` values. Indexes on `created_at` and `expires_at` support those counts and the expired-row delete. `POST /api/shared-cart` rejects a body above 8192 bytes before buffering the rest of it. It does not store an IP address, account id, or other visitor identifier. Rows store only the code, `{id, qty}` items, `created_at`, and `expires_at`. `STAGE105_SHARED_CART_LINKS.sql` is applied on the live Supabase project. RLS is forced; `anon`, `authenticated`, and `PUBLIC` have no access. `service_role` is explicitly limited on the table to `SELECT`, `INSERT`, and `DELETE`, and only `service_role` can execute the validator/create functions. Android and iOS App Links are live for `/c/*` only. The Worker serves `/.well-known/assetlinks.json` and `/.well-known/apple-app-site-association` and still denies every other dot-path. That is the released creation path. Draft PR #277 restricts creation only, and recipient import of an existing link stays on this path.
- In dark mode, cart unit price, line total, summary heading, summary figures, grand total, checkout section titles, and the member-note link use `--site-text`. Checkout placeholders use `--site-text-soft`. Light mode still uses `--site-brown-dark` and `--site-brown` for those prices and titles. That block lives in `shop.css` and was not edited again after #233.
- Public dark-mode links and account text are separate from the cart block. Back-links and in-content links on the trust pages use `--site-gold` (`#e2c98d`); visited, hover, and focus use `--site-text`. The brown `.trust-mail-btn` stays white on `--site-brown`. Shop mini-card titles use `--site-text` and their meta line uses `--site-text-soft`. The Google button and `.account-secondary` keep dark brown text on white and cream. Forgot-password, the login/signup switch, the auth kicker, and the privacy link use `--site-gold`. The divider, password toggle, and account placeholders use `--site-text-soft`. The homepage AI search button, show-more control, and meta line use `--site-text`, and their hover stays on the dark card. The password-reset page has no dark theme. Its disabled save button uses `#4b3327` on `#f4efe8` at full opacity. `404.html` stays light-only because it cannot load a script.
- Cover images on cards and the detail page use `object-fit: contain` inside a shared cream frame (`storefront-cover-presentation.css`). The image files are not cropped by that stylesheet.
- The book detail title (`.book-detail-info h1` in `book-shell.html`) is `clamp(22px, 2.6vw, 32px)` with line-height `1.3`. At `760px` and below it is `clamp(21px, 6.2vw, 28px)`. Card titles are unchanged.
- Non-numeric `/book/...` slugs are a noindex 404. `/adabiyat-roman` keeps its own title.
- Poetry and other pages load `UKIJCJK.woff2` first, then `UKIJCJK.ttf`.
- Author, translator, and publisher names on a book detail page are separate links when `book_credits` rows exist for that role. Each link opens `/author/<uuid>`, `/translator/<uuid>`, or `/publisher/<uuid>`. The same person can be an author on one book and a translator on another; those are different listings. A coauthored or jointly translated book appears under every linked person. The helper `بارلىق كىتابلىرى` uses that same destination and is not a link to only the first name. A role with no structured id stays plain text, with no helper. An empty role omits the row. A later `select=*` catalog row does not include those credit rows. `indexCatalogBook` keeps a credit array already stored for that id when the incoming book has `credits == null`. An incoming array, including an empty one, replaces it. A name with no stored identity stays plain text. Credit listings reuse the catalog cards, stock, cart, and favorites, include out-of-stock public books, and exclude inactive books with `is_active=eq.true`. They do not use the `/books` visit-order permutation. A credit query that cannot reach Supabase shows an error and does not fall back to static demo books. Legacy `books.author`, `books.translator`, and `books.publisher` stay the text columns the mobile app already reads.

## Admin and catalog behavior

- `admin.html` / `admin.js` is the admin catalog, orders, hero, and announcements UI. Admin book reads that see inactive rows require `is_kutadgu_admin()` and AAL2.
- Admin book save shows a status line in the book-modal footer (`#bookSaveStatus`). Deadlines live in `admin-save-guard.js`. Duplicate lookup 25s, exact cover fingerprint 25s, each near-dHash page 45s, cover or gallery Storage upload 180s, books INSERT/UPDATE 45s, catalog refresh 45s, cover decode 60s, WebP encode 45s. A timed-out INSERT or UPDATE is not retried; the alert tells the admin to check the book list first. After the write succeeds, the modal closes and the Save button is restored before `loadBooks()` / `loadStats()`. A failed refresh is a catalog warning, not “Save failed”.
- A new cover is decoded once. That bitmap is reused for dHash and WebP. SHA-256 still hashes the original file. Files over 50 MiB are rejected before decode. Phone photos and ordinary cover scans stay under that ceiling.
- The full near-dHash scan and distance `5` review rule stay. Gallery uploads stay serial, with “image N of M” status. `saveInFlight` and `coverFormEpoch` still block a second write and a stale cover choice.
- `book-staff.html` is the staff portal. New cover and gallery uploads go through `kutadgu-cover-image.js`: WebP quality `0.92`, longest edge `1600` px, no upscale. An in-cap image stays in its original format when the WebP is not smaller. Oversized images keep the resized WebP. Uploads use a unique path (`upsert: false`) and `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`. The same 50 MiB ceiling and 45s encode timeout apply there.
- Hide and show of inactive books stays in admin. The public storefront does not fetch `is_active=eq.false`.
- Admin and staff book forms keep the original author, translator, and publisher inputs and can add more authors or translators. Order is the field order. The same name cannot be saved twice in one role. Opening a book puts the editor in `loading` until that book's credit query returns. Save is refused while it is loading. A response for a closed, reset, or older book is ignored. Editing one role while credits load keeps that role's typed text and still fills the untouched roles from the structured rows, in stored order. A failed or missing credit query stays `blocked` or `legacyOnly`. It does not become `ready` because another field changed, and it does not call `set_book_credits`. A failed credit load still omits `author`, `translator`, and `publisher` from a later unrelated edit. When the structured write will run, the book update also omits those three columns. `set_book_credits` and `set_own_pending_book_credits` then write the links and the compatible legacy text in one database transaction. `set_own_pending_book_credits` locks the book row before it checks the current owner and pending status. `catalog_write_role_credits` locks that same book row before it deletes credit rows. Approval updates the book row and does not touch credits, so it waits on that lock. A rejected or dropped credit call does not show a completed save and does not replace the previous links. If `STAGE106` is not applied yet, the joined text is written after that missing-function result and the UI says the separate links were not. Staff submissions still go through `submit_book_for_approval`. An ambiguous legacy text change does not collapse separate contributors into one identity. If the staff credit call fails, the page stays on the retry card and does not show the approval success heading. Retry calls only `set_own_pending_book_credits`.

## Mobile app relationship

This repository is the website. It does not contain the Android app source.

`privacy.html` documents the Android app as a separate client of the same public catalog and Supabase Auth. The app can sign in with email/password or Google. Its cart, favorites, and theme stay on the device and are not synced to the member cart tables. It has no in-app card payment and no separate delivery-address form. Account deletion for the Play listing is the site page `/delete-account`.

Android package `com.kutadgubilig.kitabevi`. Production signing SHA-256 `2C:D7:46:A1:20:BA:11:D7:28:66:26:80:C7:44:C2:84:28:EF:0C:2F:D9:5C:D0:C6:6F:3F:55:E2:9A:28:44:FC`. Apple Team ID `8QU554PCLF`. iOS bundle identifier `com.kutadgubilig.kitabevi`. The canonical App Link path is `/c/*`. `.well-known/assetlinks.json` and `.well-known/apple-app-site-association.json` shipped in #239. The Worker serves the Apple file at `/.well-known/apple-app-site-association`. Production Worker `2a3f8dd4-ffb4-4386-ba3d-216b6c630e23` serves those two URLs with `Content-Type: application/json`, `GET` and `HEAD` 200, and no redirect.

EXECUTE is granted to `authenticated` only. `anon` and `service_role` cannot execute those three functions. The functions were not called by this check. After 18:00 Europe/Istanbul on 2026-10-06, authenticated calls to all three returned HTTP 200. The earlier note that the two list functions were absent, and that `get_kutadgu_analytics` lacked the cart and WhatsApp lists, described the database before this migration. A prior read-only seven-day inspection had counted 249 `search` events, 167 distinct stored `search_query` values, 127 confirmed zeros, 127 paired `zero_result_search` events, 122 positive results, 0 unknown results, and 214 `book_view` events. Those counts were not remeasured. Search reporting uses `search` events. Book views are not searches, and the paired legacy event is not a second customer search.

In the latest 24 hours Postgres recorded 20 `42501` errors, two `23505` errors, and one each of `21000` and `42P01`. There was no `57014`, `53300`, FATAL, or PANIC, and no HTTP 5xx on the API edge. Twelve `42501` rows were `permission denied for function my_book_review_notifications` from 16:06 on 2026-10-06 through 03:12 on 2026-10-07. `anon` cannot execute that function. Five were `permission denied for table analytics_events` for `ON CONFLICT ("id") DO NOTHING RETURNING 1`, the PostgREST shape of `Prefer: resolution=ignore-duplicates`, from 18:20 on 2026-10-06 through 08:29 on 2026-10-07. Current `analytics.js` sends `Prefer: return=minimal` only. Three more `42501` rows at 17:57 on 2026-10-06 were one call each to the three analytics functions. Draft PR #272 on `cursor/pending-submission-page-5fba` pages the pending staff-submission queue at 10 books and keeps a failed read from looking like an empty queue. The application commit is `a7945abf2849ee83f49fbf128c63b652130b6e90`. `admin.html` requests `admin.js?v=103`. The books read stays `submission_status = pending`, orders by newest `submitted_at` then id with null timestamps last, and takes one `.range` of 10. The exact count must be a non-negative integer, or a digit string, and the returned page length must match that count. A missing or invalid count does not fall back to the page length. Search matches title, author, and ISBN through the existing `postgrestIlike` helper and ISBN digit normalization, and it returns to page one. Changing the search invalidates the previous request immediately; the 250ms debounce is unchanged. A response is kept only when its term, page, admin identity, and request generation are still current. The cards and the pager stay on the last committed page together. A failed read keeps that page, the exact total, and the search context, shows an error, and offers `قايتا سىناش`. Logout clears the rows, total, search, timer, and retry before sign-out finishes. An account change, session loss, idle lock, or loss of AAL2 does the same, and a late response cannot restore them. Approve, reject, and edit stay the original writes. A failed refresh after a successful write says the list did not refresh and the retry only reads. An older read cannot restore a processed row. A successful refresh uses the last valid page when the old page is gone, and an edited row that no longer matches the search leaves the list. An empty queue says `تەستىق كۈتۈۋاتقان كىتاب يوق.` A search with no matches says `بۇ ئىزدەشكە ماس كىتاب تېپىلمىدى.` Neither sentence is used for a failed read.

Draft branch `cursor/admin-review-pages-5fba` pages admin reviews and replies separately, 10 rows each. `admin_book_review_page(text, text, integer)` and `admin_book_review_reply_page(text, text, integer)` return one jsonb object `{total, page, rows}`. `total` is the full status and search match. `page` is clamped to the last page, so 10 or 20 matches do not invent an empty next page. An empty match is `{total:0, page:0, rows:[]}`. Order stays oldest `created_at`, then `id`. Search trims, rejects more than 200 characters, and otherwise matches the book title or that row's stored `display_name` with `ILIKE` contains. `\`, `%`, and `_` are literal. A blank search does not filter. Reply search uses the reply's display name, not the parent review author and not `profiles`. The helper `admin_review_search_pattern(text)` is not granted. The page functions require `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal` exactly `aal2`, revoke PUBLIC and anon, and grant EXECUTE only to `authenticated`. The older `admin_list_book_reviews` and `admin_list_book_review_replies` signatures stay. `STAGE119_ADMIN_REVIEW_PAGES.sql` is additive. `STAGE119_ADMIN_REVIEW_PAGES_ROLLBACK.sql` drops only those three functions. `STAGE119_ADMIN_REVIEW_PAGES.sql` was later applied once in the PR #273 release. `STAGE119_ADMIN_REVIEW_PAGES_ROLLBACK.sql` was not run. `admin.html` requests `admin.css?v=54`, `admin-book-reviews.js?v=6`, and `admin.js?v=105`. The one `admin.js` hook calls `KutadguAdminReviews.clear()` at the end of `clearPendingSubmissionPrivate`. Pending-submission paging is unchanged. The approval bell still reads `admin_approval_counts` for the full queue. A failed read keeps the last committed rows, total, page, actions, and pager with that committed status and query. The same status and query still says `ئىنكاسلار يۈكلەنمىدى.` or `جاۋابلار يۈكلەنمىدى.`. A failed change of status or search adds `كۆرۈنۈپ تۇرغان تىزىملىك ئالدىنقى نەتىجە.`, leaves Approve and Reject on a row only when that row is pending, and disables Approve, Reject, and Delete while the committed status or query is not the selected one, including during the search wait. The old pager stays disabled. `قايتا سىناش` rereads only the failed request, and that button stays disabled unless the failed request is the selected status and query. Paging or retrying one list does not cancel or reread the other. A status change, search change, logout, idle lock, or hidden card still invalidates both. Review deletion still refreshes both lists. A confirmed approve, reject, or delete is frozen before the following session check and refresh. If that read fails, the refresh fails, or a newer read supersedes, cancels, or hides the refresh, the list keeps the success sentence and the processed row cannot be sent again. A stale refresh is not treated as a failed write. Only a later current read clears the freeze. An account change, logout, or loss of AAL2 still clears the private lists. A rejected or thrown write is not treated as success. These sentences describe draft PR #273 before it was released. The release is the baseline table above. That release is merged and deployed. It is not this empty-state draft.

Search terms and confirmed zero-result searches, with Stage 116 applied, use Europe/Istanbul calendar days taken from the snapshot `as_of`. That is the same day boundary as the reviewed zero-result contract. It is not the rolling `now() - N days` window used by the event totals, top books, cart adds, and WhatsApp clicks, and it is not the counted-visit window. Terms are grouped by trim, collapsed whitespace, and the first 80 characters. Case is not folded. Each stored `search` event counts once. A repeated completed search counts again, including inside three hours and from the same browser. A retried `event_id` stays one row. `result_count = 0` is a confirmed zero. `result_count` NULL is unknown and is not a zero. A positive count stays a successful search. When the selected window contains any `search` event, `zero_result_search` is not added to the same customer-search count. A window with no `search` event can still list legacy `zero_result_search` terms. A mixed window does not reconstruct unpaired legacy terms. Cart rankings count `add_to_cart` events. WhatsApp rankings count clicks. One click counts each distinct book once, whether the id is in `book_id` or repeated in `meta.book_ids`. Previously lost events stay lost.

First navigation to a book page can stop after the server document. That document already has the cover, title, author, and metadata. The credit fetch has no abort and no timeout. Holding any one of those requests on the local static server leaves `/book/252` (`خەنزۇچە-ئۇيغۇرچە لۇغەت`) in the server-rendered state. Those holds are controlled reproductions. They are not a capture of the customer's network. The customer screenshots were not in the workspace, so this does not identify their exact title or ISBN, and it does not explain every blank storefront cover. Live `https://www.kutadgubilik.com/book/252` on 2026-10-06 still serves `public-header.js?v=4` with `defer` and `shop.js?v=143`. This branch is not deployed and does not replace the runtime baseline above.

The visible hero image remains `src="/assets/store/shop-interior-main.webp"` with `fetchpriority="high"`. The library and exterior slides keep the same URLs in `data-hero-src` and have no `src` until the existing carousel asks for that slide. `home-hero-slideshow.js` assigns `src` in `show`, then adds `is-active` only after `naturalWidth > 0`. An image that already has `src` is not requested again. `index.html` requests `home-hero-slideshow.js?v=4` and `home-hero-content.js?v=3`. With JavaScript disabled, the first slide is still the main photo. Configured slides use the same rule: the first usable image is immediate, and later images wait. `firstDecodableIndex` probes only until the first image that can be shown, so a broken leading campaign or store image still falls through without prefetching the rest. No hero, cover, logo, or banner file was rewritten.

Cold Chromium, cache disabled, five runs, compared the live homepage with the same origin after only the hero markup and the two hero scripts were replaced. Before any transition, production requested both inactive images on every run, content-length 394,638 bytes (library 218,786 and exterior 175,852), plus the main image 266,460 bytes. The candidate requested only the main image, 266,460 bytes, and made zero inactive requests. That is the measured inactive-hero saving in this sample, about 395 KB, and it is not a whole-page saving. Desktop 1280×900 first-transition medians were 0.5 ms on production, because those images were already decoded, and 27 ms on the candidate. Phone-width 390×800 medians were 0.4 ms and 44.8 ms. Returning to the library slide added no second library request, and that transition stayed under 1 ms. Automatic advance on the candidate requested the library image only when the slide changed, at about 5.5–5.6 s, and did not request the exterior image for that first advance. LCP stayed the main hero image. After a 1.2 s settle, desktop LCP medians were 588 ms production and 564 ms candidate. Phone-width LCP medians were 520 ms and 544 ms. Desktop CLS medians were 0.011 and 0.018. Phone-width CLS was 0 in all of those runs. These are datacenter Chromium figures, including phone-width emulation, not a physical phone. A context that had already loaded `home-hero-slideshow.js?v=2` and `home-hero-content.js?v=1` requested `?v=3` and `?v=2` on the next document and still did not request the inactive images before a transition.

Contributor-link regression, deployed with PR #242. On a phone, an author name on a book page could be tapped once. After that, the name was plain text. The link was missing. Nothing covered it. The first paint loads `book_credits` and renders the anchors. A later `select=*` response, including the related-books query and the reload that refetches ids in `kutadgu-recent-v1`, `kutadgu-cart-v1`, or `kutadgu-favorites-v1`, has no credit rows. `normalizeCatalogBook` stores `credits: null`, and `indexCatalogBook` replaced the cached book. The next detail paint wrote the legacy name with no `href`. `indexCatalogBook` now copies the previous credit array only when the incoming value is `null`. An empty array stays empty, so a name with no stored identity is not given a fabricated link. Server-rendered anchors in the shell are still replaced by that client paint; they stay links when the cached book still has identities. `book-shell.html` requests `shop.js?v=143`. `credit-books.html` stays `?v=142`.

After that deploy, an existing browser session still opened the homepage with cached `shop.js?v=138` and `premium-ux.js?v=12`. Those premium cards had no `data-cover-src`. Reloading the same tab loaded `premium-ux.js?v=13`, and the same Literature cards then had `data-cover-src` with all eight covers decoded. The homepage script URL had stayed `shop.js?v=138` when the loader inside that file changed from `premium-ux.js?v=12` to `?v=13`, so the cached old loader kept requesting the old module. The user's screenshot showed blank Literature covers for books 334, 316, and 315. That blank image state was not reproduced in our browser. This branch changes only `index.html` to `shop.js?v=144`, an unused pin. `shop.js`, cover retry behavior, cache headers, and other page pins stay as they are. `.assetsignore` still lists `test-results`. A Chromium session at 1280×900 and 390×800 first cached the pre-#243 `shop.js?v=138` body, which loads `premium-ux.js?v=12`. A second document that still referenced `?v=138` did not contact the server again.
