Production Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `a2ce236a-01c4-4bd5-afff-260558bf3b8a` was deployed from that merge with `npx wrangler deploy --env production`. The cart-read application commit is `17d0e8b3ae2d772b0e61f6bd4adb682d7ac5e725`. The tested reachability commit is `18fe8e650318857323db645c9c1c4cef3a8a94ac`. The members row keeps suspend and restore and adds `سېۋەتنى كۆرۈش`. That opens `#memberCartPanel` inside the existing Members section, still after `#adminMemberList`. The panel shows the name, email, and contact already rendered for that row, then each saved title and quantity. `admin.html` requests `admin.js?v=89` and `admin-member-cart.js?v=2`. `admin.css` stays `?v=50`. Neither notification bell lists this view. Opening the panel scrolls the window only when `#memberCartTitle` or `#memberCartIdentity` sits outside an 8px viewport margin, then focuses that heading with `preventScroll`. Close and Escape restore focus to the opener when that button is still connected, enabled, and not inside a hidden ancestor. Logout, hiding the dashboard, session loss, the idle lock, and a late response do not scroll or move focus. `load`, `showRows`, and `showFailure` do not call `scrollTo`, `scrollIntoView`, or `focus`. The commit that records this paragraph does not change application behavior.

`member_cart_items` remains the only saved member cart. Guest carts stay in `kutadgu-cart-v1` on the device. The mobile app cart is still not synced. There is no new table and no change to the owner policy. An admin still cannot select or update another member's cart rows directly. `STAGE112_ADMIN_MEMBER_CART.sql` adds `admin_member_cart_page(uuid, text)`. The function is security definer, stable, and `search_path = public`. It requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal = aal2`, rejects a null member, and returns only `book_id`, `quantity`, and `title`. A missing book keeps the quantity and a null title. An inactive book still returns its stored title because the function reads as definer. Rows are ordered by `book_id` in pages of 100. Execute is granted to `authenticated` only. Public and anon are revoked. The function grants no write. Rollback is `STAGE112_ADMIN_MEMBER_CART_ROLLBACK.sql` and drops only that function. It was not run. Before this release the function was absent and no `stage112` migration was recorded. `STAGE112_ADMIN_MEMBER_CART.sql` from the merge commit was applied once as `stage112_admin_member_cart` `20261005101204`. The live function body matches that file, including `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal` exactly `aal2`. Execute is false for `anon` and `public`, and true for `authenticated`. The function ACL is `{postgres=X/postgres,authenticated=X/postgres}`. A call without an admin AAL2 JWT returns `42501` `Admin permission required`. A call as `anon` returns `42501` `permission denied for function admin_member_cart_page`. The only `member_cart_items` policy remains `cart owner access` for `authenticated`, `FOR ALL`, with `user_id = auth.uid()` and `is_member_active()`. Table grants were the same before and after. No new cart-write privilege was added. The saved-row count and quantity total were the same before and after. No customer cart was written.

A loading panel says `سېۋەت يۈكلىنىۋاتىدۇ...`. A successful empty page says `سېۋەتتە كىتاب يوق.`. A failed or rejected read says `سېۋەتنى ئوقۇش مەغلۇپ بولدى.` and keeps `قايتا سىناش`. It is not shown as an empty cart. A blank or null title says `كىتاب ئۇچۇرى يوق` and still shows that row's quantity. A later failed refresh of the same member keeps the last complete page and says `يېڭىلاش مەغلۇپ بولدى.` plus `ئالدىنقى كۆرۈنۈش ساقلىنىۋاتىدۇ.`. Switching members clears the previous rows before the next read. A late response is ignored after another member is selected, the panel is closed, logout, the dashboard is hidden, the session is gone, or the idle lock is shown. Admin AAL1 does not call the read. The panel is not stored in `localStorage` or `sessionStorage`. The script does not log cart rows.

Isolated Postgres, not the live project, ran `node scripts/stage112-isolated-verify.js` on `17d0e8b3ae2d772b0e61f6bd4adb682d7ac5e725`. It was not repeated on the reachability commit because the SQL did not change. Anonymous execute and anonymous table select were denied. An ordinary member still read only their own rows and could not execute the function, including at AAL2. Admin AAL1 was denied. Admin AAL2 could not select or update the table directly, and the function returned the other member's saved quantities, an inactive title, a blank title, and a null title for a missing book. One hundred and one rows came back as 100 then 1, ordered by `book_id`. A member with no rows returned an empty page. Rollback removed the function and left the owner read in place. Reapply restored the admin read. Saved quantities were unchanged before the reads, after them, and after rollback and reapply.

The commit that records this paragraph does not change application behavior. Members search, account status, and saved-cart filters run in `admin_member_directory_page(text, text, text, integer)` before a 20-row page. Search matches `full_name`, `email`, `phone`, `country`, and `city`. `%`, `_`, and `\` are literal. Normal means `status` is distinct from `suspended`. The cart filter is an `EXISTS` on `member_cart_items`, so several cart rows do not duplicate a member. Order is `created_at desc nulls last, id desc`. The page and the count share one filtered set. Dashboard totals stay global and still exclude the signed-in admin. A row's order count and total still use only `confirmed`, `processing`, `shipped`, and `completed` after trim and lower-case. `admin.html` requests `admin.js?v=91` and `admin-member-cart.js?v=3`. `admin.css` stays `?v=50`. A page that is past the end after suspend or restore steps back to the last page that has rows. Idle lock, logout, account switch, and loss of AAL2 clear the member rows and the open cart. A rejected directory read rechecks the current admin and AAL2 before it keeps any rows. The last successful page stays only for that same AAL2 admin. Another account's failed read drops the previous rows, the displayed member statistics, and the open cart instead of labeling them as its own previous result. Order names are written into the profile cache only when that same admin is still at AAL2, the members surface is open, and that cache map has not been replaced. A late profile response after the idle lock, an account switch, sign-out, or loss of AAL2 is discarded. If paging or filtering removes the open member, `syncList` closes that cart instead of leaving it under another name. `STAGE113_ADMIN_MEMBER_DIRECTORY.sql` from that merge is applied on `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh` as `stage113_admin_member_directory` `20261005131355`. A delete confirmation names the book title and an excerpt of the selected review or reply, then describes that dependent deletion. The admin header bell counts pending staff book submissions, pending reviews, and pending replies. Approving someone else's reply notifies the parent review's author.

A failed or rejected approved-review read keeps the last hearts, public replies, and own reply status for that member and book. Only a successful empty response clears them. The error and retry stay visible, and the screen does not invent a zero heart count or an empty reply list. A refresh requested while a reply insert is in flight runs for the member who is current when the insert finishes, including after sign-out. A notification read requested during another member's in-flight read, or when the tab becomes visible again, runs after the older result is discarded. The same queue applies to admin approval counts. A successful reply insert whose following refresh fails keeps a local saved and pending note until an authoritative read sees that reply, and it does not insert that same text again. Retry is read-only. A failed heart write shows `ياقتۇرۇش يوللانمىدى. قايتا سىناڭ.` and the button can be used again.

Opening a notification loads the approved reply and its approved parent through `public_book_review_reply_target(uuid)` when they are outside the first 50 reviews or 200 replies, including a move from another page to the book. The function is security invoker, does not call `is_kutadgu_admin()`, and does not return user ids. The reply text is rendered and reached before the notification is marked read. A missing, deleted, or inactive-book target shows `بۇ جاۋاب تېپىلمىدى.` and does not change the URL or report the notice as read. Admin review and reply lists use a stable keyset of 100, ordered by `created_at` then id, for pending, approved, and rejected. The requested filter, load generation, and page cursor are captured before the await. Next and Previous do not change the committed page until that page's read succeeds. A returned error or a rejected read keeps the rows and controls of the last successful page and shows the read-failure text. Retry requests that same destination cursor. A missing later cursor is not sent as the first page. Review and reply page positions stay independent. A late response from another filter, from after sign-out, or from after the idle lock is ignored. Sign-out and the idle lock clear the private lists and the approval bell.

`my_book_review_status(bigint)` still returns only the row for `auth.uid()`. Anonymous readers still see only approved reviews of active books, and that read does not call `is_kutadgu_admin()`. A failed review read still shows `باھالار يۈكلەنمىدى.` and `قايتا سىناش` without claiming there are no reviews. Typed text and the last valid review state stay in place. Switching members or signing out drops the older response.

No-result pagination is one browsing snapshot. The first call and Refresh use the selected 7/30/90 value, omit `p_as_of`, and start at offset 0. `get_kutadgu_zero_searches` stamps `as_of` at `now()` and derives the Europe/Istanbul day window from that instant. Load more sends the retained session's days, the same `as_of`, and `next_offset`. It does not read the date control again. Grouping, order, and totals include only `created_at <= as_of`. A search that arrives after `as_of`, including a repeat that would become the newest row, stays outside the snapshot, so a group that has not been loaded yet remains reachable. An append response whose days, snapshot time, calendar bounds, cursor, or totals do not match the retained session is rejected. The rows, totals, and range label already on screen stay as they were. The forward file drops the older three-argument function before creating the four-argument one, so Postgres does not keep both. A call that omits `p_as_of` still starts a snapshot because that argument defaults to NULL. A future `as_of` is clamped to `now()`. The payload `schema_version` for this function is 2. That is not the Stage 100 analytics schema. This Load more fix did not change the SQL.

The shared 7/30/90 control is the admin's selection. Neither loader writes it. If one request fails, that section keeps the data it already showed and the label names that range, including the shown and total query counts. The other section can show the newly selected range at the same time. A stale response does not repaint the newer selection.

Visit lifecycle for the unfiltered All books listing:

- A visit is one tab session. `sessionStorage` key `kutadgu-books-visit-v1` stores `{seed, ids, cursor}`. A new tab or a new browser session starts empty and receives a new seed. The seed is not written to `localStorage`. If `sessionStorage` throws, the same visit stays in module memory until that document is discarded.
- The snapshot is one Fisher-Yates permutation of the canonical active ids collected when the visit first builds it. The same seed and the same id list always produce the same unique order. A different seed moves books across the whole catalog. Books added after the snapshot join the next visit.
- Pagination walks that frozen list. Each step requests only the full records needed to fill the current page, in batches of at most 100 ids. If that response is shorter than the request and `Content-Range` shows more rows, those later rows are read before any unresolved id is treated as missing. A deleted or hidden id is then skipped and still consumes a snapshot position, so later ids are not dropped and Load more ends when the cursor reaches the snapshot end. A short HTTP 206 with no total fails the listing. Reloading a loaded span is capped at 5000 rows in one response.
- Sort, search, collection, price, and reset during Load more abort that append and run the new query. A second Load more click while the first append is in flight is ignored. A response from the aborted request does not repaint the grid, move the visit cursor, or clear the newer request’s loading state.
- The id index selects `id,is_active` (or `id` when `is_active` is missing), ordered by `id`, in pages of 1000. A short page is complete when `Content-Range` gives the total, or when the response is HTTP 200. A short HTTP 206 with no total fails the listing. A configured remote error stays on the existing retry message and does not show static demo books.
- The default label is `بۇ قېتىملىق بايقاش تەرتىپى` (`discover`). Search uses relevance. Newest, title, author, price, bestseller, and recommended stay explicit. Price, collection, category, and hub filters leave rotation. Returning to the default sort in the same visit restores the same snapshot and the cards already loaded. Book detail and Back keep that progress through `sessionStorage` and BFCache.

That run applied it twice, loaded 24 representative rows, applied it again with the row count unchanged, then checked access, Istanbul boundaries, visitors, duplicates, and joins. Rollback left 24 rows and the new columns, and applying the forward file again restored `schema_version` 2. Measured on that run: Istanbul today `2026-10-01` was partial with 3 visitors, 5 events, and 4 identified events; yesterday was complete with 1 visitor; the 7-day period distinct count was 3. The user-action funnel had 2 carts and the arrival funnel had 2 carts with `accurate_user_action_order` false. Book 15 and book 2 each had 1 view. Whether `STAGE100` has been applied to the live project is still unknown.

## Asset and cache pins

Query pins are how cached storefront files change. Bump the pin when the file’s behavior changes. Do not put `immutable` on an unhashed `?v=` URL.

| File | Pin at the runtime baseline |
|---|---|
| `shop.js` | `book-shell.html`, homepage, listings, `credit-books.html`, and the other storefront documents updated in PR #266 request `?v=147`. `cart.html` stays `?v=141`. `shop.js` requests `kutadgu-book-views.js?v=6`, `app-config.js?v=6`, and still loads `premium-ux.js?v=13`. |
| `catalog-credits.js` | `?v=3` on `admin.html` and `book-staff.html`. `book-shell.html` and `credit-books.html` stay `?v=1` |
| `catalog-credits.css` | `?v=1` on `book-shell.html` and `credit-books.html` |
| `kutadgu-shared-cart.js` | `?v=3` on `cart.html` only |
| `kutadgu-visit-order.js` | `?v=2` on `books.html`, immediately before `kutadgu-search-rank.js` |
| `kutadgu-book-seo.js` | `?v=6` on `book-shell.html` and on the storefront documents that previously requested `?v=3`. |
| `supabase-config.js` | `?v=22` |
| `member.js` | `?v=28` |
| `admin.js` | `?v=86` (`no-store`; role-aware credit loading and one contributor transaction) |

| `book-staff.css` | `?v=8` |

`vercel.json` cache, last matching header wins:

- General `/(.*)\.(css|js)`: `public, max-age=300, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400`
- After that rule, `no-store, must-revalidate` for `/admin.js`, `/catalog-bibliography.js`, and `/supabase-config.js`
- `UKIJCJK.woff2`: `public, max-age=3600, s-maxage=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800` (`scripts/security-hardening-2a-tests.js`)
- HTML such as `admin.html` and `book-staff.html` is `no-store`

Several SEO tests freeze SHA-256 values for `shop.js`, `index.html`, `book-shell.html`, and `vercel.json`. Behavior edits to those files must refresh the frozen hashes and the Stage 2H allowlist in `scripts/stage-seo-2h-category-listing-seo-tests.js`.

## Testing

Unit tests:

```bash
npm run test:unit
```

Stage 10 is `.github/workflows/stage10-regression.yml`: `npm ci`, Playwright Chromium, then `npx playwright test`. On a pull request the workflow refuses to fall through to production. Local equivalent, against this checkout rather than production:

```bash
unset KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL
export KUTADGU_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173
export KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC=1
npx playwright test
```

`playwright.config.js` uses `KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL`, then `KUTADGU_BASE_URL` / `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL`, then `https://www.kutadgubilik.com`. Leave the preview URL unset when the local server should be the target. The local server is `scripts/static-preview-server.js` on `127.0.0.1:4173`.

Stage 10 CI is Chromium only. Use Firefox and WebKit when a change is visual or browser-specific.

Do not send live analytics or view-count writes while measuring production traffic. Abort non-GET/HEAD Supabase calls in that kind of measurement.

## Security and RLS constraints

- Anonymous and normal authenticated clients can read active books only (`SUPABASE_SETUP.sql`, policy `public can read active books`).
- Admin reads of all books, profiles, orders, and analytics require `is_kutadgu_admin()` and AAL2 where that policy says so.
- `get_kutadgu_analytics` stays granted to `authenticated` only. The live function, read on 2026-09-30 before #218 merged, was still the older rolling-window body: it returns page, book, cart, and WhatsApp totals plus `top_books` and `zero_searches`. It does not return visitor counts or the cart, WhatsApp, and search breakdowns. `STAGE100_ADMIN_DAILY_VISITORS.sql` is the manual replacement. Do not apply `STAGE8_STORE_ANALYTICS.sql` over it. Neither #218 nor the visit-order work ran that migration on the live project. The isolated local run is recorded with the previous baseline note above. Merging #218 does not by itself change the live function.
- `public.match_active_books_ai` is the AI Search matcher. `STAGE_AI_SEARCH_1G2_CATEGORY_LOOKUP.sql` does not replace that function. `STAGE_AI_SEARCH_RPC_SERVER_ONLY.sql` leaves both function bodies as they are. It grants EXECUTE only to `service_role` and revokes EXECUTE from `anon`, `authenticated`, and PUBLIC on `match_active_books_ai(extensions.vector, integer)` and `list_active_books_by_categories_ai(text[], integer)`. That grant is live. Production Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `5d6fb126-8971-4c56-8f6b-8acb5feb56a6` is serving it, and a normal AI search returns HTTP 200. On both AI RPCs, `service_role` EXECUTE is true. `anon`, `authenticated`, and PUBLIC EXECUTE are false. A direct anon call returns permission denied. The public publishable key remains the browser catalog key. Do not put the server key in Git, HTML, browser JS, Android, iOS, responses, or logs.
- Admin and storage writes stay on the admin/staff paths. Do not weaken RLS, grants, or Storage policies to make a public page work.
- Password recovery must use the redirect and `token_hash` flow noted in `supabase-config.js`, not `{{ .ConfirmationURL }}`.
- `scripts/security-hardening-2a-tests.js` rejects `eval`, `new Function`, and string timers in app HTML/JS.

## Do not touch

- Database passwords, service-role keys, Vercel tokens, GitHub tokens, `.env` values, and customer PII. They do not belong in git or in this file.
- Production rows, Storage objects, and Auth users, unless the task explicitly includes a reviewed migration.
- Logo, font, and cover paths. `README.md` says they are tied to the database and static catalog.
- Existing SQL history. Add a new idempotent file when a migration is actually required. Do not rebuild `SUPABASE_SETUP.sql` in place.
- `STAGE11_RECOVERY.md`’s rule that a Vercel rollback is not a database rollback.
- Bulk rewrite of existing cover objects. Upload optimization applies to new uploads.
- `immutable` cache on unhashed `?v=` assets.

## Known intentional behavior

- The public inactive-book request is gone on purpose. Admin hide/show remains.
- Two homepage card groups may still request `book_view_stats` separately. Same-id and same-set calls are deduped. Different id sets are not merged.
- The detail page may read a book’s view stats once for display and once more after a successful engagement so the visible total can move.
- Cover presentation does not crop or replace image files. Odd proportions stay letterboxed inside the frame.
- An in-cap JPEG, PNG, or WebP is not replaced by a WebP that is the same size or larger.
- Admin cover files above 50 MiB are rejected before decode. The stored image is still capped at 1600px.
- This supabase-js 2.45.4 build does not forward `abortSignal` on Storage `upload`. The admin UI stops waiting at 180s. The HTTP upload may still finish later as an unused object because the path is unique and `upsert` is false.
- Category listing HTML is packaged for the Vercel function. `includeFiles` paths must stay within Vercel’s length limit (#201).
- Guest carts are device-local. They are not in Postgres.

## Admin analytics

The Analytics card in `admin.html` reads `get_kutadgu_analytics`. Until `STAGE100_ADMIN_DAILY_VISITORS.sql` is applied by hand, visitor counts and the new breakdowns show as unavailable (`—`), not zero. After it is applied, deploy the site. The site can ship first: inserts omit `visitor_id`, `event_id`, `host`, `occurred_at`, and `action_seq` if those columns are missing, and also `legacy_id` and `meta`. Each recognized optional column is omitted once. That budget is separate from the lost-response retry, so five missing columns on the first page view still reach one valid insert. A lost response or HTTP 5xx is retried once only while `event_id` is still on the request, so the unique index can ignore the duplicate. After `event_id` is omitted, that same failure is dropped instead of inserted again.

Definitions once the migration is applied:

- Time zone is `Europe/Istanbul`. The selected 7, 30, or 90 days are calendar days ending today, not a rolling `now() - N days` window. The chart is always the last 7 Istanbul days, including today.
- A visitor is one anonymous browser id stored in `localStorage` (`kutadgu-analytics-visitor`). The tab session id stays in `sessionStorage`. Several pages or reloads on one Istanbul day count as one visitor. Page views stay page views.
- Today, yesterday, and the selected period each use their own `count(distinct visitor_id)`. The period total is not the sum of the daily totals.
- Days with no events are zero. Days with events but no visitor id are unavailable. Days with a mix count only the identified visitors and are marked partial. Historical rows are not rebuilt from page-view totals.
- The user-action funnel orders `action_seq`, which the browser assigns once when the person acts and reuses on retry. The server keeps `occurred_at` only from 5 minutes before `now()` through 1 minute after; otherwise it clears both ordering fields and still stores the event. A later step must have a strictly greater sequence, so equal timestamps and equal sequence numbers are not a later step. Sessions without that pair are excluded, not reconstructed from arrival time. `arrival_funnel` uses `created_at` and sets `accurate_user_action_order` false. The old RPC has no funnel object, so the page labels those figures as aggregate event ratios and does not cap them at 100%. WhatsApp is intent, not a sale.
- A search with an unknown result count is unknown, not a zero-result search. `search` and `zero_result_search` are not added together.
- No-result admin list, after `STAGE101_ADMIN_ZERO_SEARCHES.sql`: `get_kutadgu_zero_searches(p_days, p_offset, p_limit)` returns one jsonb page. It does not replace `get_kutadgu_analytics`. Days are Europe/Istanbul calendar days, clamped to 1..365. Page size is clamped to 1..50. The browser asks for 20. Order is last searched time descending, then the normalized query ascending.
- A qualifying row is `event_name = 'search' AND result_count = 0` when the selected window contains any `search` event. `result_count` NULL is unknown, not zero. `zero_result_search` is used only when that window has no `search` event at all, and only when its `result_count` is NULL or 0. The two names are never added for the same window, because the current site writes both for one completed zero search and the legacy table has no `event_id` to pair them. A legacy-only query inside a mixed window is omitted. That is a documented limit, not a reconstructed history. There is no exactly-once claim while `event_id` is absent.
- Grouping matches browser normalization: trim, collapse whitespace, keep 80 characters, do not fold case. The partial index `analytics_events_zero_search_recent_idx` limits the canonical scan to confirmed zero `search` rows in time order. It is not an expression group index. The legacy event still uses `analytics_events_name_created_idx`.
- The function requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and AAL2. Execute is granted to `authenticated` only. Public and anon are revoked. A missing function shows a setup-required state and an em dash, not the old top-ten list and not zero. An empty confirmed period shows zero. A failed read keeps the previous rows when they exist and does not paint a failed read as zero. Refresh and a date change start again at offset 0. A stale response does not repaint a newer selection.
- Until Stage 100 is applied, the other analytics cards still use the rolling window in the live `get_kutadgu_analytics`. Their zero-result total can disagree with this calendar list. This section uses only the total from `get_kutadgu_zero_searches`.
- Homepage, header, `/books`, and category search record after the current request succeeds. The recorded query is the text captured when that request started. Load more, a redraw, an aborted or stale response, a blank query, a sensitive query, an unknown total, and a failed catalog request do not add a zero-result event. A later deliberate repeat of the same search can increase the count. A successful search still records `search` only. The homepage premium empty-state enhancer can rewrite a failed search's `.search-empty` box into the no-results sentence. That failure is still not recorded.
- Book lists resolve a numeric id first, then the lowest `books.id` for a legacy id. They do not join on both at once. WhatsApp book ids are deduplicated per click.
- `book_view`, `book_engagement_detail`, and `add_to_cart` stay separate. Analytics does not change `books.sales_count`.
- Collection skips local, preview, and other non-production hosts, and skips `admin.html` and `book-staff.html`. A logged-in admin on the public storefront can still be counted, because inserts use the public key. Bot filtering is not complete. PostHog stays separate and is not added to these totals.

Apply order: run `STAGE100_ADMIN_DAILY_VISITORS.sql` in the Supabase SQL editor, then deploy. Rollback is `STAGE100_ADMIN_DAILY_VISITORS_ROLLBACK.sql`, run by itself. It restores the previous rolling-window function and the Stage 99 insert policy, drops the timing trigger, and leaves `visitor_id`, `event_id`, `host`, `occurred_at`, and `action_seq` in place. Do not delete `analytics_events`. Do not replace the function with `STAGE8_STORE_ANALYTICS.sql`.

## Cloudflare parallel preview

This is not a runtime baseline move. `vercel.json`, the Vercel `api/*.js` routes, Supabase, Auth, RLS, and production image URLs stay as they are. PR #222 merged to `main` at `1bbdd2bd`. The remote preview is `https://kutadgu-cloudflare-preview.kutadgu-preview.workers.dev`. It binds R2 bucket `kutadgu-covers-preview` as `COVERS`. Live `www` is served by `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` through Worker Custom Domains and reads covers through the private `/__r2/book-covers/` route. Supabase remains the stored image URL and the fallback. The implementation note is `CLOUDFLARE_MIGRATION_REPORT.md`. That cutover left the runtime baseline at `445fe488`. The current baseline is the cart text commit in the table above.

- A Workers preview serves the same static site and calls the existing book, category, sitemap, and AI search modules. It does not replace the Vercel deployment. `npm run preview:cloudflare` runs that preview with Wrangler state outside the repo so the asset watcher does not reload on its own writes.
- The preview worker refuses `www.kutadgubilik.com` and `kutadgubilik.com`. The preview top-level Wrangler config has no production hostname. Production custom domains are only under `env.production`. The retired Vercel alias is not an allowed production host.
- R2 cover bytes for the 1020 active public Supabase cover and gallery objects were copied into `kutadgu-covers-preview` and checked with SHA-256. The workers.dev preview now displays those covers through the private Worker route. Production, Vercel, and the database still use Supabase URLs. `KUTADGU_R2_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` is empty, r2.dev stays disabled, and the admin upload route stays off. A future public R2 host can be named with `KUTADGU_R2_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` without removing the Supabase host.
- Storefront pins are unchanged. This branch does not change shop, admin, or book-shell behavior.
- Final pre-production QA compared the workers.dev preview with `https://www.kutadgubilik.com` before cutover. The preview still returns 421 for production hosts, serves private R2 only on workers.dev, and leaves AI search disabled. Production `POST /api/ai-search` is enabled on the production Worker. - Remaining production step: none for the already deployed cart view. The website is deployed. Do not run `apply_catalog_credit_corrections()` again. Three unmatched review rows remain, listed in the live SQL status. `catalog_credit_correction_map` already has forced row level security, and the repository file now contains those two statements. Rollback is `STAGE106_CATALOG_CREDITS_ROLLBACK.sql`, run by itself. It drops the credit policies before `book_credits`, then the new tables, functions, and trigger. It does not change `books.author`, `books.translator`, `books.publisher`, or book rows.

## How to resume work safely

1. Read this file, `README.md`, and, if the task can touch data or deploys, `STAGE11_RECOVERY.md`.
2. `git fetch origin main`. Compare `git rev-parse origin/main` with the runtime baseline. They may differ. Do not treat the stored SHA as the current `main` tip.
3. If a PR recorded here has since merged, reconcile this file with `origin/main` before new work. Documentation-only commits on `main` still leave the runtime baseline where it is.
4. Create one branch from current `main`. Recent agent branches look like `cursor/<descriptive-name>-c4dc`. Open one draft PR. Do not merge it unless the task says to merge.
5. Keep the diff inside the task. Do not mix schema, RLS, Auth, Storage, or production data into a storefront or docs change.
6. For storefront or admin behavior, run `npm run test:unit`. Run Stage 10 when the change can affect pages Playwright covers. Refresh frozen hashes and the Stage 2H allowlist when those tests require it.
7. Before the task is considered complete, update this file in that same PR. A later session confirms the merge. Do not open a second documentation PR just to move a SHA.

## Economics and food categories

This branch adds two book categories to the existing catalog list. `books.category` and `books.source` stay free text. There is no new table, constraint, or permission change, and no existing book row is rewritten. No SQL file is added.

The labels are appended after `گرامماتىكا`:

- `ئىقتىساد` uses source `iqtisad.html`, slug `iqtisad`, and route `/iqtisad`
- `تائاملار` uses source `taamlar.html`, slug `taamlar`, and route `/taamlar`

Admin category options still sort with `localeCompare("ug")`. Staff options stay in the config order. Homepage cards, the search category list, category listing, sitemap, Vercel rewrites, and the Worker `run_worker_first` paths follow the existing hub pattern. An empty listing uses the existing sentence `بۇ بۆلۈمدە ھازىرچە كىتاب يوق.` Out-of-stock books stay visible with the existing stock badge. Inactive books stay out of the public listing.

Storefront documents that requested `shop.js?v=146` now request `shop.js?v=147`. `book-shell.html` requests `shop.js?v=147` so the detail category link resolves. `cart.html` stays `shop.js?v=141`. `shop.js` requests `app-config.js?v=6`. `admin.html` and `book-staff.html` request `app-config.js?v=6`. `analytics.js?v=7` and `kutadgu-book-views.js?v=6` are unchanged. `.js` cache headers are unchanged. A browser that already cached `shop.js?v=146` and `app-config.js?v=5` fetches `shop.js?v=147` and `app-config.js?v=6` on the next document that asks for those pins.

`kutadgu-book-seo.js` now includes `iqtisad` and `taamlar`. The deployed helper does not. Every browser script tag for that file requests `kutadgu-book-seo.js?v=6`. That covers the 113 documents that requested `?v=3` and `book-shell.html`, which requested `?v=5`. Node and Worker imports load the file directly and have no query pin. A browser that already cached `?v=3` or `?v=5` fetches `?v=6` on the next document that asks for it. Category canonical URLs from the helper are `https://www.kutadgubilik.com/iqtisad` and `https://www.kutadgubilik.com/taamlar`. Book breadcrumb structured data uses those same URLs.

The Android and iOS apps are not in this repository. They already read `books.category` and `books.source` as text, so a book assigned to either label fits the public catalog contract without a schema change. A category menu hardcoded inside the app will not list these hubs until that app is updated. This change does not edit the app.

PR #268 on `cursor/admin-visit-countries-5fba` is merged and deployed. The reviewed head stayed `00771930a69542fb3838ff0a690e621151dad7f8`. The review fixes for coverage gaps, rejected analytics reads, auth-context invalidation, and the cached-recorder check are in that tested commit. The merge commit is `2d4cd477448bf32b0ee0a2c78da182d541d32af5`, merged 2026-10-08T21:51:34Z through the normal merge path. No admin override was used. Its parents are `c2e42dcdfb5f46604e780c9d49bd5146ee902908` and `00771930a69542fb3838ff0a690e621151dad7f8`, and its tree matches the reviewed head. `npx wrangler deploy --env production` from that merge, Wrangler 4.145.0, deployed Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `d95dfbe2-d5a8-42c1-86fc-e98cd4a05f66`, created 2026-10-08T21:52:10.051846Z. The version listing records the source as an upload and does not store the git SHA. The earlier Worker `16145b30-be1b-460d-bdd1-f1cc5a3f0d41` was the previous production version. No production test user, visit, book, review, or order was created. No live admin AAL2 session was opened.

Stage 10 run [37845082298](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/37845082298), job `113543828515`, completed 2026-10-08T21:26:15Z on the tested application commit: 876 passed, 3 skipped. The country specs in that run passed. The earlier run [37838266341](https://github.com/kutadgubiligkitab/kutadgu-bilig-kitabevi/actions/runs/37838266341) on `1ac75efaedd7a6009a0a4e59033341fdcfa86c5c` finished 2026-10-08T20:31:17Z with 873 passed, 3 skipped, and 1 flaky. That run is before the review fixes. The flaky case there was `tests/e2e/book-reviews.spec.js` (“signed-out readers see approved text and a compact invitation”), which passed on retry.

The counted-visit gate is still `private.kutadgu_accept_page_visit`. The first public page view counts. A later page view counts only when the last counted visit is at least three hours earlier. A suppressed page view does not move that window. Midnight does not reset it. The same `event_id` is one receipt, including a retry or a concurrent duplicate. `/admin.html` and `/book-staff.html` stay excluded. Book views, cart adds, searches, and other events stay stored and are not counted. These figures are counted page views, not verified people or unique visitors.

Country comes from Cloudflare `request.cf.country` on `POST /api/analytics-event`. The Worker drops a country or address supplied in the body or in `CF-IPCountry`. It inserts with the server secret and `Prefer: return=minimal`. The response is empty and `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. A valid `A-Z` code other than `XX` is stored for a public `page_view`. `XX`, `T1`, and any other invalid value stay unknown (`NULL`). The database trigger clears `country` unless the inserting role is `service_role` and the row is a public page view, so a direct anon insert cannot spoof one. An update keeps the original country. A new counted receipt copies that country. A duplicate `event_id` returns the existing receipt and does not change it. A suppressed receipt stores no country. The event row can still hold the code from that insert; the report does not read it.

`private.analytics_country_counter.started_at` is set on the first apply and is not moved by applying the file again. It is separate from `private.analytics_visit_counter.started_at`. There is no backfill. A window that ends at or before the country marker is unavailable, including an empty one. A window that crosses it is partial and shows only receipts from the marker forward. Inside the covered slice, a public page view with no receipt, or one without a usable visitor id or event id, is also partial. Accepted totals and unknown-country receipts stay in that result. Unknown metadata on a counted receipt is a measured unknown, not a processing gap. A suppressed receipt is not a gap. A fully covered quiet interval, or one with only suppressed visits, is a measured zero. Totals and the latest 20 rows come from the same counted receipts. The latest 20 are newest first and omit visitor ids, event ids, and addresses.

`public.get_kutadgu_analytics` keeps the Stage 116 body and adds `visit_countries`. It still requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal = aal2`. `anon`, authenticated non-admins, AAL1 admins, and `service_role` cannot read it. The admin page reuses `#analyticsRange` (7, 30, or 90 Istanbul calendar days). Labels are Uyghur. A short note says the country is the network location and can differ when a VPN is used. A returned error or a thrown request keeps the last successful numbers and their period, leaves the loading state, and can be retried. `routeSession` drops a pending country read when the session is missing or the account is not an admin. An account id change does the same before the next read can be stored. Logout invalidates that read before `signOut` resolves, including when `signOut` is slow or rejects. A network error while an admin is already signed in does not clear the report. Authorization checks are unchanged.

Storefront documents that requested `analytics.js?v=7` now request `analytics.js?v=8`. `admin.html` requests `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=10` and `admin.js?v=96`. Storefront pages stay on `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=5`. `admin.css` stays `?v=51`. `.js` cache headers are unchanged. A local Chromium context loaded a same-origin page that requested `analytics.js?v=7` with the previous recorder body. `cache: only-if-cached` returned that body. A second load of the same page reused it with transfer size 0 and did not ask the server again. The next navigation, without a reload or a cache clear, requested `analytics.js?v=8` once and did not run the old recorder. This was not a physical phone.

If country collection fails, the Worker answers 503 and the browser stores the same event with the existing direct insert. That visit still counts, with country unknown. A missing `country` column is retried once without the column, so the site can ship before the SQL. Browsing, the three-hour gate, and non-page-view events stay in place. Geolocation is not on the page-rendering path. There is no external geolocation service, map, country block, or shipping integration.

The site and Worker were deployed before the SQL. Migration `20261008215715`, name `stage117_visit_countries`, applied `STAGE117_VISIT_COUNTRIES.sql` from the merge once. `private.analytics_country_counter.started_at` is `2026-10-08 21:57:15.454826+00`. `private.analytics_visit_counter.started_at` stayed `2026-10-06 08:31:56.449593+00`. The country counter has row level security and no policy. `anon` cannot execute `get_kutadgu_analytics` or `kutadgu_country_visit_report`, and cannot select `analytics_events` or the country counter. A live anonymous call returned HTTP 401, `42501`, permission denied for the function, and the same denial for the events table. `authenticated` can execute `get_kutadgu_analytics`; the function still requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal = aal2`. `service_role` cannot execute it. `anon` and `authenticated` cannot execute `kutadgu_accept_page_visit`. The accept function still contains the three-hour interval and copies a normalized country onto a new counted receipt. Existing counted receipts were not backfilled: 148 counted receipts remained, and both events and receipts with a non-null country were 0 at verification. No public page view was stored from the Worker deploy at 21:52:10Z through the read. An empty `POST /api/analytics-event` returned HTTP 400 with an empty body and `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. `GET` returned HTTP 405 and `Cache-Control: no-store`. Neither request stored a row.

Live `analytics.js`, `admin.js`, and `kutadgu-analytics-core.js` match the merge. Their SHA-256 values are `9e05b5d4143836f4a28dfaa0a3200abe121b1b4bf35ec2ee1fb75d9f40aa1081`, `20c4515c937a96a0fd78c3333001e5f282b9c5b83aa4c7605c29b7487b165421`, and `75189c49ead00fb7631a6240e2f82a7935b63a678fe67ead8e3f10426eb9d19d`. `analytics.js` uses `public, max-age=300, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400`. Exact `/admin.js` stays `no-store, must-revalidate` by the existing security header. Live `/` requests `analytics.js?v=8` and `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=5`. `admin.html` requests `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=10`, `admin.js?v=96`, and `admin.css?v=51`. `book-shell.html` requests `analytics.js?v=8` and `shop.js?v=147`. Historical visits from before the country marker stay out of the totals. A VPN or shared network can show a different country from the person's location.

## Admin counted-visit country history pagination

This change was deployed as PR #271. That release is no longer the runtime baseline. The current baseline is the table above, the PR #256 merge `9a77c7eeab930e582d2ed7a35b12718703e9c6a0`, Worker `kutadgu-cloudflare-production` version `e3c9e3ad-0be3-4eb3-900e-d1997a1220ff`. The reviewed head `96d211ad4e0378e35fe24fdf87277b83b83fc10b` has the same tree `1a8b890e6962c01a678535c48e965cda0b10a055` as the PR #271 merge. The tested application commit for that history work is `fcccdbc3add800af71a4f006c24dd5d25762aa9d`. The earlier `main` commit `54193145ae751bb50a8e9bfa4afa8f3710dbcff6` only records the PR #270 release. It is not the current deployed application.

`public.get_kutadgu_visit_country_history(p_days, p_offset, p_limit, p_as_of, p_snapshot)` is new. It does not replace `get_kutadgu_analytics` or the latest-20 preview inside `private.kutadgu_country_visit_report`. The admin panel still loads country totals from that report and pages them in the browser. History is a separate request. Search and the totals pager do not call it. A failed first history read stays on screen when those totals controls are used, and retry still repeats that history read.

The history window is the existing Europe/Istanbul calendar, anchored at `p_as_of` or at `now()` when `p_as_of` is omitted. A future `p_as_of` is clamped to `now()`. `counted_at <= as_of` only bounds that window. It is not a database snapshot. The counting trigger still passes `now()`, which is the inserting transaction's start time, so a transaction that begins before the first page and commits after it can store a receipt whose `counted_at` is already inside the window. The function does not change that trigger, `counted_at`, receipt retention, or the three-hour gate. Unknown countries stay. Suppressed receipts stay out. The default limit is 20 and the maximum is 50. Offset is clamped to 0–1000000. Days follow the existing 1–365 clamp. A window that ends at or before the marker is unavailable, with `rows` and `total` null. It is not reported as zero.

The first page and an explicit refresh omit `p_snapshot`. The function then copies the `event_id` of every counted receipt this transaction can already see inside the window into `private.kutadgu_visit_history_snapshot_members`, and stores the window on `private.kutadgu_visit_history_snapshots`. An uncommitted insert is not in that copy. Later pages send the opaque `snapshot_id` and read only the copy, so the late commit cannot change the total or shift rows. The id is not an event id, visitor id, or transaction id. `event_id` is not returned. Coverage is measured once, with the same marker and gap rule as the country report. The migration role owns both tables and the function. The function is the only reader and writer. RLS is on, there are no policies, and `anon`, `authenticated`, and `service_role` have no table privileges. Every call still requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal=aal2`. A snapshot id does not bypass that check. Creating a snapshot deletes snapshot rows older than 36 hours and their member ids. That delete does not touch events, receipts, gates, or markers. A later page whose id is missing fails and leaves the last page in place. A successful period change clears the old snapshot and reads page one of the new period. A failed first read for that period shows an error and retry, not a fabricated zero and not the previous period’s rows. Country-total search and its pager do not replace that error with the latest-20 preview. A failed page change or refresh keeps the last rows, page, period, and snapshot. Retry repeats the failed operation. A second page click while a request is in flight is ignored. A period change, logout, session loss, or account change invalidates an older response.

If the function is not in the schema cache, the panel keeps the latest-20 preview and the note that it is not the full history. The open or closed state of the panel is left as the admin set it across refresh and period changes. Logout and an account change collapse it and clear the rows. There is no polling and no download of the whole history.

A failed history page keeps its rows, snapshot, period, and error note. A later `get_kutadgu_analytics` refresh that returns an error, or whose promise rejects, keeps that history page and leaves `#analyticsVisitCountryRetry` visible. That retry repeats the failed history page, including the retained snapshot and offset. The analytics failure stays on the analytics status line, and `↻ يېڭىلاش` still retries that read. A successful history read clears only the history failure. A successful analytics read clears the analytics failure notice. Logout, session loss, and an account change still clear both.

`admin.html` requests `admin.css?v=53`, `admin.js?v=101`, and `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=13`. Storefront pages stay on `kutadgu-analytics-core.js?v=5`. The history heading is `تاللانغان ئارىلىقتىكى زىيارەت خاتىرىسى`. The page indicator is `1 / 2 · جەمئىي 21` style, isolated left to right. The preview heading remains `ئاخىرقى 20 سانىلىدىغان زىيارەت`.

Migration `STAGE118_VISIT_COUNTRY_HISTORY.sql` creates the function and the two private snapshot tables. It is `SECURITY DEFINER` with `search_path = public`, uses schema-qualified tables, requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal = aal2`, grants execute only to `authenticated`, and revokes execute from `PUBLIC`, `anon`, and `service_role`. Rollback is `STAGE118_VISIT_COUNTRY_HISTORY_ROLLBACK.sql`, run by itself. It drops the function and both snapshot tables. It does not delete `analytics_events` or `analytics_visit_receipts`, does not move either collection marker, and does not rewrite Stages 114, 115, 116, or 117.

Safe later release order: apply `STAGE118_VISIT_COUNTRY_HISTORY.sql` first, then deploy the admin assets. The SQL does not change collection or the existing analytics function. Deploying the assets first is also safe, because a missing function keeps the accurate latest-20 preview. Do not run the rollback as part of a later change. `STAGE118_VISIT_COUNTRY_HISTORY.sql` from the merge was applied once as `stage118_visit_country_history` `20261009231420`. Rollback was not run.

Local proof used PostgreSQL 16 and PostgREST 14.5 on throwaway databases. `scripts/stage118-isolated-postgres.sh` applied Stages 114 through 118, repeated the apply without moving markers or rows, denied anon, `service_role`, an ordinary member, and admin AAL1, and accepted admin AAL2. The assertions covered a quiet zero, one row, a 20-row page, 21 rows across two pages and across three limit-10 pages, equal timestamps, an unknown country, a suppressed visit, a new arrival outside an open snapshot, Istanbul midnight, the country-marker boundary, a partial gap, and an unavailable window. `scripts/stage118-snapshot-race.sh` then began a real `page_view` insert before the first history page, left it uncommitted while that page was read, committed it, and walked the later pages with the retained snapshot id. `counted_at` was the inserting transaction’s `now()` and was inside the window anchor. The retained total stayed 23 across 2 pages, with no duplicate or omitted row and without the late country. A new snapshot included it. Rollback and a second apply kept the events, receipts, and both markers, and removed the function and both snapshot tables. `scripts/stage118-isolated-postgrest.sh` repeated the denial and the admin AAL2 read through PostgREST, built the next page with `kutadgu-analytics-core.js`, and checked that a receipt inserted between requests did not enter the open snapshot. The same run then repeated the uncommitted-insert race through PostgREST and that client helper. The retained total stayed 24. The missing-function response after rollback and the unchanged September total after reapply still held.

Playwright used Chromium at 390×800 and 1280×900 against the local static server. The history list showed separate country, date, and time columns, previous and next controls, and the page indicator. Country-total search and paging stayed on their own request. After a failed first history read, typing in the country-total search and using its pager left the error and retry in place, and retry repeated that history read. A failed page change and a failed refresh kept the last rows. A failed period change showed an error rather than the old rows or a zero. A stale page did not replace the new period. Logout and an account change dropped a late response. Without the RPC, the latest-20 preview and its limited note remained. After a failed history page, a later analytics refresh that returned an error, and one whose promise rejected, each left the history page, its error note, and the history retry in place at both viewports. The retry sent the retained snapshot and offset and loaded the next page. The analytics failure stayed on the analytics status line. A second failed analytics refresh did not bring the history retry back. A successful analytics refresh cleared that notice. `node scripts/visit-country-tests.js`, `node scripts/three-hour-visits-tests.js`, and `node scripts/cover-upload-optimize-tests.js` passed. Playwright `tests/e2e/admin-visit-countries.spec.js` and `tests/e2e/admin-three-hour-visits.spec.js` passed, 27 tests. `scripts/stage-seo-2h-category-listing-seo-tests.js` still fails its pre-existing `iqtisad` and `taamlar` assertions. The frozen-file check passed. This was desktop Chromium, not a physical phone, and not a live AAL2 session. No image, cover, logo, or banner file was modified.

## Homepage discovery loading-space reservation

PR #256 on `cursor/discovery-cover-space-5fba` is merged and deployed. The merge is `9a77c7eeab930e582d2ed7a35b12718703e9c6a0`, and it is the runtime baseline in the table above. They are not a second baseline.

`showGroup` paints eight empty card slots only while a discovery group is loading. The slots reuse the current `.premium-book-card` and 2:3 `.premium-card-cover` geometry. They sit in `.premium-book-grid[aria-hidden="true"]` and have no image, link, book id, favorite control, or cart id. The placeholder cart control is disabled and `tabindex="-1"`. The existing sentence `كىتابلار يۈكلىنىۋاتىدۇ…` stays a sibling inside `#premiumDiscoveryResults` (`aria-live="polite"`). `discovery-cover-space.css?v=1` places that sentence over the grid without adding height. A success, shorter list, empty result, or error replaces the pending markup, so the eight slots do not remain. `discoverySeq` and `AbortController` are unchanged. The wizard still uses the short `discoveryLoadingMarkup()` spinner.

Homepage `index.html` requests `shop.js?v=148`. `shop.js` loads `premium-ux.js?v=14` and `/discovery-cover-space.css?v=1`. Other storefront documents stay `shop.js?v=147`. `cart.html` stays `shop.js?v=141`. `analytics.js?v=8` and `kutadgu-book-seo.js?v=6` are unchanged. `premium-ux.css` stays `?v=10`. No extra unavailable-mark rule was added. The current cover box already contains that mark at the measured widths.

The read-only audit on `d68e38a0` measured the jump this reservation addresses. An injected 900ms book delay, a local cover, both themes, `dir=rtl`, and the results in view gave a 150px loading sentence. Eight plain cards were 1567px at 390×800, 2599px at 768×900, and 1165px at 1280×900. Cover boxes were 147×220, 312×468, and 266×399. Eight recommended badges grew further by 124px, 124px, and 62px. Two cards were 385px, 639px, and 575px. Empty and failed results returned to the prompt height, 116px or 87px. Holding images at `naturalWidth` 0 left the grid height unchanged, and decoding the local logo did not move it. That audit does not show that naturally blank covers until reload are fixed. This change does not claim to fix that symptom or to make image downloads faster.

Frozen `shop.js` SHA-256 is `6425f2c1c222e47286e74f761fd6c98f8f683bdbb6258c04568ab01d6cf99053`. Frozen `index.html` SHA-256 is `07a565e767b6078d13856797c9265c55d75f42e4659cb90586e719017fc7d54f`.

## Members-only private website feedback

PR #275 merged to `main` at `2eff3e230b814cc7b0ddc08012b28482c67dccbe` on 2026-10-11T23:18:28Z. The release facts are in the account-controlled cart sharing section above. Draft PR #276 is not that deploy. The paragraphs below were written while PR #275 was still a draft.

Draft PR #275 on `cursor/member-website-feedback-5fba` adds a private suggestion page. The base is `origin/main` `b6d0aa793f814934110e5d51dfb99b4236a7f5fd`, the PR #274 merge. An older paragraph above still calls that empty-state work an unmerged draft. That sentence is stale. The application commit in the merge is `a996827b7e3cdf4fa6a576272994b88016ba3de3`. `0f57135076d54bed11735993c4cf503ff13528ae` is the Stage 10 record in the merge. `ef7488ac5eb81ba8daac94e6cddb63ce040de98a` is not an ancestor of `origin/main` and is not the deployed baseline. The merge's admin pins are `admin-book-reviews.js?v=7`, `admin.css?v=54`, and `admin.js?v=105`. The first application commit is `dcac6edf4a830fc2f5759fd3459e8d45988e5580`. The first review corrections are application commit `03c2ac45c2d65ea4808c9f906674cc41de5bdf48`. The remaining review corrections are application commit `4d945d18f2738836000f65612b7aa2874796c570`. The test revision on that same tree is `570d8b128a6c0dffdceb4a461491eb51a670c60b`. The commit that recorded this section was documentation only and did not move the runtime baseline. The following status described the draft before the merge recorded above. `STAGE120_WEBSITE_FEEDBACK.sql` is applied on Supabase project `fxlojnqwyojqjskfggmh` as migration `stage120_website_feedback` version `20261011231703`. The isolated grant proof below was the throwaway-database proof from that draft.

The public footer link is `پىكىر ۋە تەكلىپ` and opens `/feedback`. `shop.js` `siteFooterHtml()` is the shared footer. `account.html` has its own footer and received the same link. Every storefront HTML file that loads `shop.js`, including `cart.html` and `book-shell.html`, requests `shop.js?v=151`. `v=148` is the homepage pin on `origin/main`. `v=149` and `v=150` were earlier pins on this draft. `account.html`, `book-staff.html`, `feedback.html`, and the dynamic loader in `shop.js` request `member.js?v=30`. `v=28` and `v=29` are earlier member pins. `feedback.html` requests `feedback.js?v=3` and `feedback.css?v=1`. `admin-feedback.js` stays `v=2` because that file did not change in this pass. Frozen SHA-256 values for this correction are `shop.js` `85c9148477e9b3067130c57fefd01c2ff4f867d9f09b6725c567e4cf202e185b`, `index.html` `67522ac0c612f6cd19f8fc4ef07b1ae503697e2d719beb5104166007e5409cdd`, and `book-shell.html` `883ebe37475474be1cd73a66cee7499f983ed8ed18510f2738f8a78dce9f6176`. The page is `noindex` and is not in the public sitemap.

Signed-out visitors see one invitation to `/account.html`. The 2,000-character limit counts Unicode code points, the same measure as PostgreSQL `char_length`. The textarea `maxlength` is 4000 so 2,000 supplementary characters can be typed. The counter, the submit gate, and the database check use code points. Both ends drop the same whitespace, including vertical tab, non-breaking space, and the other separators in `website_feedback_normalize`. Internal spaces and line breaks stay. A whitespace-only message is rejected in the browser and in `submit_website_feedback`. A suspended profile cannot submit. The sender is `auth.uid()`. A repeated id with the same normalized body returns the stored row. A different body for that id raises `23505` and does not replace it. `member.js` calls `noteAuthUser` from `onAuthStateChange` before the session queue. The form stays hidden until that auth user has an established active profile. A delayed member event for another account does not restore the previous draft or show its success. Each submission owns a `sendSeq`. `finishSend` changes the busy flag, button, status, receipt, or draft only when that same request is still current. An older completion, whether it returns an error or throws, leaves the newer member's pending submission alone. A repeated submit while that request is pending does not send again. `beginSignOut()` runs before `auth.signOut` and clears the draft. `signOut` otherwise matches `origin/main`: it abandons the in-memory shop sync, awaits `auth.signOut({ scope: "local" })`, and does not snapshot or restore the member, profile, cart, favorites, owner, or merge lock. A thrown rejection stops before the shared clear finishes, so an identity that arrived during the wait is not replaced by the old account. A resolved `{ error }` is not treated as a saved-session restore; the shared clear still finishes, matching main. Feedback stays closed until a later auth event establishes an eligible active profile. Compared with `origin/main`, the only `member.js` additions are `noteAuthUser` and `beginSignOut`.

`public.website_feedback` has RLS enabled and forced, no policies, and no table privileges for `anon`, `authenticated`, or `service_role`. The submit and page functions are security definer with `search_path = public`. After the migration, `authenticated` can execute those two functions. `public`, `anon`, and `service_role` cannot. `website_feedback_normalize` is not executable by `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`, or `service_role`. In the isolated database, default privileges would have left `service_role` able to execute new functions; the migration revokes that. That revoke was proven only on the throwaway database. `admin_website_feedback_page(integer, timestamptz, uuid)` requires `is_kutadgu_admin()` and JWT `aal` exactly `aal2`. Page 0 with both cursors null is unbounded and returns `bound_at` and `bound_id` for its newest row. Later pages in that walk pass both values, so a row inserted after the walk started stays out of it and appears on the next unbounded page 0. A page 1 request that omits the cursor still repeats the previous last row and skips the oldest row when a newer row arrives. The admin client sends the cursor after the first response and clears it when the section opens. One cursor without the other raises `22023`. The function returns `member_label` and not `user_id`. Ordinary members cannot select, count, or page the rows, including their own. Deleting `auth.users` cascades only that member's rows. Reapplying the migration after rows exist does not delete or change those rows. `STAGE120_WEBSITE_FEEDBACK_ROLLBACK.sql`, run alone, drops the table before `website_feedback_normalize`, then drops both page signatures and the submit function. That deletes every stored suggestion. It does not change profiles, reviews, replies, carts, favorites, or analytics.

The admin section is `💬 پىكىرلەر`. It shows 10 rows, newest `created_at` then `id`. An empty inbox says `پىكىر يوق.` once, in the status region. An auth change to another admin, a lost AAL2 session, or sign-out clears the retained rows immediately. A failed read keeps the previous rows only for the same still-authorized AAL2 admin. A delayed `getSession` that returns an older admin does not repaint that inbox. `42501` clears the rows. `admin.html` requests `admin-feedback.js?v=2` and `admin.js?v=106`. `admin-book-reviews.js?v=7` and `admin.css?v=54` stay. Reviews, view counts, covers, cart, favorites, and analytics are unchanged.

Local proof on test revision `570d8b128a6c0dffdceb4a461491eb51a670c60b`, whose application parent is `4d945d18f2738836000f65612b7aa2874796c570`, at 2026-10-11T22:22:03Z: `node scripts/stage120-isolated-verify.js` exited 0 with 66 passes and 0 failures on embedded PostgreSQL and PostgREST. `node scripts/website-feedback-tests.js` exited 0. Playwright at `http://127.0.0.1:4193`, with `KUTADGU_PREVIEW_URL` and `KUTADGU_USE_LOCAL_STATIC` unset, passed `tests/e2e/website-feedback.spec.js`, `tests/e2e/website-feedback-warm-cache.spec.js`, and `tests/e2e/admin-feedback.spec.js`: 20 passed. The warm-cache spec preloaded `shop.js` at `?v=148`, `?v=149`, `?v=150`, and `?v=142`, `member.js` at `?v=28` and `?v=29`, and `feedback.js` at `?v=2`, then opened `/`, `/books`, `/book-shell.html`, `/cart.html`, `/account.html`, and `/feedback` and loaded `shop.js?v=151`, `member.js?v=30`, and `feedback.js?v=3` without a hard reload. The member specs use real `member.js`. They hold A's submission, establish B, hold B's submission, and then reject or return an error for A while B stays pending. They also cover delayed sign-out, a thrown `auth.signOut`, a resolved `{ error }`, an identity change before the delayed failure, and `SIGNED_OUT` before that failure. No old cart or favorites snapshot returns. `tests/e2e/account-cross-tab-logout.spec.js` and `tests/e2e/account-header-cart-count.spec.js` passed, 15 tests. The admin inbox spec returns `bound_at` and `bound_id`, inserts a newer row between pages, checks both cursor arguments on the next request and on retry, and shows that newer row only on the next unbounded page 0. Viewports were 390×800 and 1280×900, with `dir=rtl`, in Chromium. That is emulation, not a physical phone, and not a live AAL2 session. The full `npm test:unit` list has 138 scripts. A rerun on this tree had 107 passes and 31 failures. Each of those 31 also exits non-zero on `origin/main` `b6d0aa793f814934110e5d51dfb99b4236a7f5fd`. No unit script that passes there fails on this tree. They include Stage SEO 2H `iqtisad` and `taamlar`, `scripts/admin-order-management-tests.js` test 4, and the `scripts/admin-book-staff-tests.js` `Promise.all` pattern. `scripts/homepage-compact-ux-tests.js`, `scripts/stage82-stock-foundation-tests.js`, `scripts/stage83-stock-enforcement-tests.js`, `scripts/book-view-counts-tests.js`, and the `shop.js?v=148` check in `scripts/stage98-search-relevance-tests.js` stop on this branch at that historical pin because `shop.js` is `v=151`. On `origin/main` those scripts fail a different pre-existing check: `public-header.js?v=2`, `app-config.js?v=5`, a missing Playwright module, or the search-rank script order on `cart.html`. The SQL-name guards that previously failed only by naming the two Stage 120 files now allow exactly `STAGE120_WEBSITE_FEEDBACK.sql` and `STAGE120_WEBSITE_FEEDBACK_ROLLBACK.sql` and still reject other SQL. No image, cover, logo, or banner file was modified. ## How to update this document

Update it in the same PR as a significant feature, bug fix, or infrastructure change, before that PR is considered complete. Skip trivial formatting-only edits. Documentation-only commits do not change the runtime baseline.

Record the feature or fix, the PR number when it exists, the tested branch and head SHA, and the tests or status. Update pins, behavior, and future work when those changed. Move the runtime baseline only when application or runtime behavior changed. Delete notes that are no longer true.

A later session checks whether that PR merged and reconciles this file with `origin/main`. Never assume the stored baseline is the current `main` tip.

Do not paste secrets, tokens, passwords, `.env` contents, or service-role keys. Public domains and the public Supabase ref may stay because they are already in the repo.

If a new file is added, `scripts/stage-seo-2h-category-listing-seo-tests.js` may need that path on its allowlist. This file and `.cursor/rules/project-recovery.mdc` are already listed.
